      const matches=references.filter(reference=>SCOPES[reference.scope]).map(reference=>{const comparison=compare(source,reference),policy=SCOPES[reference.scope],dormant=reference.scope==='PERMANENT'&&Number(reference.runsSinceLastUsed)>=3,qualityException=allowDormantQualityVariant&&dormant&&source.qualityUpgradeApproved===true,sameTriggerInCurrentRun=['EQUIPPED','RUN_OWNED'].includes(reference.scope)&&comparison.parts.trigger===1;return{referenceId:reference.referenceId,scope:reference.scope,runsSinceLastUsed:reference.runsSinceLastUsed,weightedScore:round(comparison.rawScore*policy.weight),sameTriggerInCurrentRun,blocked:(comparison.exact||sameTriggerInCurrentRun||comparison.rawScore>=policy.blockThreshold)&&!qualityException,...comparison}}).sort((a,b)=>Number(b.blocked)-Number(a.blocked)||b.weightedScore-a.weightedScore||String(a.referenceId).localeCompare(String(b.referenceId))),blocking=matches.find(item=>item.blocked),maxWeightedScore=matches[0]?.weightedScore||0,candidate={...clone(source),similarity:{maxWeightedScore,closestMatches:matches.slice(0,3)},adjustedRankScore:round(Number(source.rankScore||0)-maxWeightedScore*30)};
      if(blocking)rejected.push({...candidate,rejection:{reason:blocking.exact?'EXACT_MECHANISM_DUPLICATE':blocking.sameTriggerInCurrentRun?'SAME_TRIGGER_IN_CURRENT_RUN':'TOO_SIMILAR_TO_EXISTING_PERSONA',referenceId:blocking.referenceId,scope:blocking.scope,rawScore:blocking.rawScore}});else accepted.push(candidate);
    }
    accepted.sort((a,b)=>b.adjustedRankScore-a.adjustedRankScore||a.id.localeCompare(b.id));
    const selected=[],selectedIds=new Set(),byFamily=new Map();
    for(const candidate of accepted){const family=candidate.mechanismFamilyId||'AI_FAMILY_OTHER';if(!byFamily.has(family))byFamily.set(family,[]);byFamily.get(family).push(candidate)}
    const families=[...byFamily.keys()].sort();let depth=0;
    while(selected.length<maxCandidates&&families.some(family=>byFamily.get(family)[depth])){for(const family of families){const candidate=byFamily.get(family)[depth];if(candidate&&!selectedIds.has(candidate.id)){selected.push(candidate);selectedIds.add(candidate.id);if(selected.length>=maxCandidates)break}}depth++}
    for(const candidate of accepted){if(selected.length>=maxCandidates)break;if(!selectedIds.has(candidate.id)){selected.push(candidate);selectedIds.add(candidate.id)}}
    return clone({schemaVersion:1,id:`AI_SIMILARITY_RESULT_V1:${pool.runtimeNodeId}:${pool.directionId}`,runtimeNodeId:pool.runtimeNodeId,directionId:pool.directionId,sourceCandidateCount:pool.candidates.length,acceptedCount:accepted.length,rejectedCount:rejected.length,candidates:selected,rejected});
  }
  root.AiPersonaSimilarity=Object.freeze({SCOPES,compare,collectReferences,filterPool,mechanism});
})(globalThis);


// SOURCE: persona/ai/template-factory.js
(function(root){
  'use strict';
  const clone=value=>value==null?value:JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(value));
  const safe=value=>String(value||'AI').replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9_-]/g,'_');
  // 人格牌配图（策划分类 → 游戏内方向）：桥接=桥接，偏转=破局，映照=顺势。
  // AI 人格生成/读档时按内部分类随机取一张，写入 template.portrait 随存档持久化（不在渲染时取，避免闪变）。
  const AI_PERSONA_ART_POOLS={
    AI_DIRECTION_BRIDGE:['personas-v2/ai-bridge-01.png','personas-v2/ai-bridge-02.png','personas-v2/ai-bridge-03.png','personas-v2/ai-bridge-04.png','personas-v2/ai-bridge-05.png','personas-v2/ai-bridge-06.png','personas-v2/ai-bridge-07.png','personas-v2/ai-bridge-08.png','personas-v2/ai-bridge-09.png','personas-v2/ai-bridge-10.png','personas-v2/ai-bridge-11.png'],
    AI_DIRECTION_BREAK:['personas-v2/ai-deflect-01.png','personas-v2/ai-deflect-02.png','personas-v2/ai-deflect-03.png','personas-v2/ai-deflect-04.png','personas-v2/ai-deflect-05.png','personas-v2/ai-deflect-06.png','personas-v2/ai-deflect-07.png','personas-v2/ai-deflect-08.png','personas-v2/ai-deflect-09.png','personas-v2/ai-deflect-10.png','personas-v2/ai-deflect-11.png'],
    AI_DIRECTION_FOLLOW:['personas-v2/ai-mirror-01.png','personas-v2/ai-mirror-02.png','personas-v2/ai-mirror-03.png','personas-v2/ai-mirror-04.png','personas-v2/ai-mirror-05.png','personas-v2/ai-mirror-06.png','personas-v2/ai-mirror-07.png','personas-v2/ai-mirror-08.png','personas-v2/ai-mirror-09.png','personas-v2/ai-mirror-10.png','personas-v2/ai-mirror-11.png']
  };
  function randomPortraitFor(directionId){const pool=AI_PERSONA_ART_POOLS[directionId]||[];return pool.length?`assets/art/${pool[Math.floor(Math.random()*pool.length)]}`:null}
  root.AiPersonaPortraitPool=Object.freeze({randomPortraitFor,poolFor:directionId=>AI_PERSONA_ART_POOLS[directionId]||[]});
  function create(whitelist){
    if(!whitelist||whitelist.id!=='TARGET_AI_PERSONA_WHITELIST_V1')throw new Error('AI persona template factory requires the V1 whitelist');
    const effectById=new Map((whitelist.mainEffectParts||[]).map(item=>[item.id,item]));
    function fromCandidate(candidate,{sequenceNumber,portrait=null,templateId=null}={}){
      if(!candidate||candidate.status!=='LOCAL_LEGAL_CANDIDATE')throw new Error('AI persona template factory requires a legal candidate');
      if(!Number.isInteger(sequenceNumber)||sequenceNumber<whitelist.temporaryNaming.sequenceStart)throw new Error('AI persona sequenceNumber is invalid');
      const number=String(sequenceNumber).padStart(whitelist.temporaryNaming.minDigits,'0'),name=`${whitelist.temporaryNaming.prefix}${number}`,effect=effectById.get(candidate.components.mainEffectPartId),runtime=clone(candidate.runtimeTemplate);
      if(!effect)throw new Error('AI persona candidate main effect is unknown');
      return{
        id:templateId||`AI_PERSONA_${number}_${safe(candidate.id).slice(-14)}`,
        displayId:name,
        name,
        code:`AI_PERSONA_${number}`,
        entryId:null,
        mainAttributeId:effect.mainAttributeType,
        mainEntry:candidate.playerCopy.trigger,
        mode:'人格',
        mainEffect:{triggerText:candidate.playerCopy.trigger,effectText:candidate.playerCopy.mainEffect,growthText:candidate.playerCopy.growth},
        conditions:runtime.conditions,
        effects:runtime.effects,
        qualityId:'PROTOTYPE',
        behaviorFamilyId:candidate.mechanismFamilyId||null,
        activationLimit:runtime.activationLimit,
        growthRules:runtime.growthRules,
        caps:runtime.caps,
        runtimeDefaults:runtime.runtimeDefaults,
        runtimeScopes:runtime.runtimeScopes,
        subAffixRules:{id:whitelist.affixPolicy.id,schemaVersion:whitelist.affixPolicy.schemaVersion,slotCount:whitelist.affixPolicy.slotCount,defaultUnlockedCount:whitelist.affixPolicy.defaultUnlockedCount,unlockCosts:clone(whitelist.affixPolicy.unlockCosts),poolIds:clone(whitelist.affixPolicy.poolIds||[]),slotPoolIds:clone(whitelist.affixPolicy.slotPoolIds||{}),disallowSameAttributeType:whitelist.affixPolicy.disallowSameAttributeType===true,allowDuplicates:whitelist.affixPolicy.allowDuplicates,candidatePoolStatus:whitelist.affixPolicy.candidatePoolStatus,maxAttributeCount:3,maxSubAttributeCount:2},
        tags:['AI人格'],
        decisionStatus:'PROTOTYPE_ASSUMPTION',
        tone:'#725A3A',
        portrait:portrait||randomPortraitFor(candidate.directionId),
        aiPersonaMeta:{schemaVersion:1,playerFacing:false,sourceCandidateId:candidate.id,runtimeNodeId:candidate.runtimeNodeId,internalDirectionId:candidate.directionId,mechanismFamilyId:candidate.mechanismFamilyId||null,mechanismFingerprint:candidate.mechanismFingerprint,components:clone(candidate.components),behaviorTags:clone(candidate.behaviorTags),budgetMetrics:clone(candidate.budgetMetrics),playerCopy:clone(candidate.playerCopy)}
      };
    }
    return Object.freeze({fromCandidate});
  }
  root.AiPersonaTemplateFactory=Object.freeze({create});
})(globalThis);


// SOURCE: persona/ai/generator.js
(function(root){
  'use strict';
  const clone=value=>value==null?value:JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(value));
  const EARLY=['AI_DIRECTION_BRIDGE','AI_DIRECTION_BREAK'];
  function create(whitelist){
    if(!root.AiPersonaCandidateBuilder||!root.AiPersonaSimilarity||!root.AiPersonaTemplateFactory)throw new Error('AI persona generator dependencies are missing');
    const builder=root.AiPersonaCandidateBuilder.create(whitelist),factory=root.AiPersonaTemplateFactory.create(whitelist),policyByNode=new Map((whitelist.nodePolicies||[]).map(item=>[item.runtimeNodeId,item]));
    function assignDirection({runtimeNodeId,assignments={},randomValue=0}={}){
      const policy=policyByNode.get(runtimeNodeId);if(!policy)throw new Error(`Unknown AI persona node: ${runtimeNodeId}`);
      const next=clone(assignments||{}),existing=next[runtimeNodeId];if(existing){if(!policy.directionIds.includes(existing))throw new Error(`Saved AI persona direction is invalid at ${runtimeNodeId}`);return{directionId:existing,assignments:next,created:false}}
      if(policy.directionMode==='FIXED'){next[runtimeNodeId]=policy.directionIds[0];return{directionId:next[runtimeNodeId],assignments:next,created:true}}
      const otherNode=runtimeNodeId==='N04'?'N08':'N04',other=next[otherNode];
      if(other&&EARLY.includes(other)){next[runtimeNodeId]=EARLY.find(id=>id!==other);return{directionId:next[runtimeNodeId],assignments:next,created:true}}
      const order=Number(randomValue)>=.5?[EARLY[1],EARLY[0]]:EARLY;next.N04=order[0];next.N08=order[1];return{directionId:next[runtimeNodeId],assignments:next,created:true};
    }
    function libraryFallback(fallbackTemplates,runtimeNodeId,references){
      const ranked=(fallbackTemplates||[]).filter(item=>item?.id).map(template=>({template,maxSimilarity:(references||[]).reduce((max,reference)=>Math.max(max,root.AiPersonaSimilarity.compare(template,reference).rawScore),0)})).sort((a,b)=>a.maxSimilarity-b.maxSimilarity||a.template.id.localeCompare(b.template.id)),selected=ranked.find(item=>item.maxSimilarity<.95);
      return selected?{ok:true,kind:'LIBRARY_PERSONA',reason:'NO_DISTINCT_LOCAL_CANDIDATE',runtimeNodeId,templateId:selected.template.id,template:clone(selected.template),maxSimilarity:selected.maxSimilarity}:{ok:false,kind:'NO_RESULT',reason:'NO_DISTINCT_LOCAL_OR_LIBRARY_CANDIDATE',runtimeNodeId};
    }
    function prepare({snapshot,directionId,handTypes=[],references=[],maxCandidates=12}={}){
      const rawPool=builder.build({snapshot,directionId,handTypes,maxCandidates:Math.max(96,maxCandidates*8)}),filtered=root.AiPersonaSimilarity.filterPool(rawPool,{references,maxCandidates});
      return{runtimeNodeId:snapshot?.runtimeNodeId,snapshotId:snapshot?.id||null,directionId,rawPool,filtered};
    }
    function generate({snapshot,directionId,handTypes=[],references=[],selectedCandidateId=null,sequenceNumber,portrait=null,fallbackTemplates=[],maxCandidates=12,prepared=null}={}){
      const preparedPool=prepared||prepare({snapshot,directionId,handTypes,references,maxCandidates}),{rawPool,filtered}=preparedPool;
      if(!filtered.candidates.length)return libraryFallback(fallbackTemplates,snapshot?.runtimeNodeId,references);
      const selected=filtered.candidates.find(candidate=>candidate.id===selectedCandidateId)||filtered.candidates[0],selectionSource=selectedCandidateId&&selected.id===selectedCandidateId?'AI_SELECTED':'LOCAL_FALLBACK',template=factory.fromCandidate(selected,{sequenceNumber,portrait});
      return{ok:true,kind:'GENERATED_PERSONA',reason:selectionSource==='LOCAL_FALLBACK'?(selectedCandidateId?'INVALID_AI_SELECTION':'NO_AI_SELECTION'):null,runtimeNodeId:snapshot.runtimeNodeId,directionId,selectionSource,selectedCandidateId:selected.id,template,candidate:clone(selected),trace:{snapshotId:snapshot.id,rawCandidateCount:rawPool.candidateCount,distinctCandidateCount:filtered.acceptedCount,rejectedSimilarityCount:filtered.rejectedCount}};
    }
    return Object.freeze({assignDirection,prepare,generate});
  }
  root.AiPersonaGenerator=Object.freeze({create});
})(globalThis);


// SOURCE: persona/ai/generation-service.js
(function(root){
  'use strict';
  const clone=value=>value==null?value:JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(value));
  function create(whitelist,{generator}={}){
    const runtimeGenerator=generator||root.AiPersonaGenerator?.create(whitelist);
    if(!runtimeGenerator?.prepare||!runtimeGenerator?.generate||!root.AiPersonaBehaviorSnapshot?.assertValid)throw new Error('AI persona generator service dependency is missing');
    function assignDirection(options){return runtimeGenerator.assignDirection(options)}
    function prepare(options={}){
      const safeSnapshot=root.AiPersonaBehaviorSnapshot.assertValid(options.snapshot),generationInput={...options,snapshot:safeSnapshot},preparedPool=runtimeGenerator.prepare(generationInput),requestId=`AI_PERSONA_SELECTION_V1:${safeSnapshot.runtimeNodeId}:${safeSnapshot.id}`,candidates=(preparedPool.filtered?.candidates||[]).map(candidate=>({id:candidate.id,playerCopy:clone(candidate.playerCopy),behaviorTags:clone(candidate.behaviorTags||[]),budget:clone(candidate.budget||null)}));
      const request={schemaVersion:1,requestId,runtimeNodeId:safeSnapshot.runtimeNodeId,directionId:options.directionId,behaviorSnapshot:safeSnapshot,candidateIds:candidates.map(candidate=>candidate.id),candidates};
      return{schemaVersion:1,request,generationInput:clone(generationInput),preparedPool};
    }
    function selectedIdFrom(preparation,response){
      if(!response||typeof response!=='object')return null;
      if(JSON.stringify(Object.keys(response).sort())!==JSON.stringify(['requestId','selectedCandidateId'])||response.requestId!==preparation.request.requestId||typeof response.selectedCandidateId!=='string')return'__INVALID_AI_SELECTION__';
      return preparation.request.candidateIds.includes(response.selectedCandidateId)?response.selectedCandidateId:'__INVALID_AI_SELECTION__';
    }
    function finalize(preparation,response=null){
      if(!preparation?.request||!preparation?.preparedPool)throw new Error('AI persona selection preparation is invalid');
      const selectedCandidateId=selectedIdFrom(preparation,response),result=runtimeGenerator.generate({...preparation.generationInput,prepared:preparation.preparedPool,selectedCandidateId});
      return{...result,selectionEnvelope:{schemaVersion:1,requestId:preparation.request.requestId,responseAccepted:result.selectionSource==='AI_SELECTED',fallbackUsed:result.selectionSource!=='AI_SELECTED'}};
    }
    function generateLocal(options){return finalize(prepare(options),null)}
    return Object.freeze({assignDirection,prepare,finalize,generateLocal});
  }
  root.AiPersonaGenerationService=Object.freeze({create});
})(globalThis);


// SOURCE: battle/score-runtime.js
(function(root){
  'use strict';

  function create({pokerEngine,shopRuntime,personaRuntime,stageLimitRuntime=null,personaFeedback,minScore=1,maxSelection=5}={}){
    if(!pokerEngine||!shopRuntime||!personaRuntime||!personaFeedback)throw new Error('BattleScoreRuntime requires poker, shop, persona and feedback runtimes');
    const clean=value=>Number(Number(value).toFixed(8)),display=value=>String(Number(Number(value).toFixed(2)));

    function resolve({cards,consume=false,handTypes,growthState={},stageLimitInstance=null,stageLimitView=null,context={}}={}){
      const evaluation=pokerEngine.evaluate(cards,handTypes,maxSelection),growth={suitChipBonusBySuit:{},handTypeLevelsById:{},...growthState},handLevel=Number(growth.handTypeLevelsById?.[evaluation.typeId]||0),handChipBonus=Math.round(evaluation.chips*.1)*handLevel,handMultBonus=Number((evaluation.mult*.1*handLevel).toFixed(4)),baseHandChips=evaluation.chips+handChipBonus,baseHandMult=evaluation.mult+handMultBonus,events=[{phase:'牌型',source:evaluation.type,detail:`${evaluation.chips} 筹码 ×${evaluation.mult}`,chipsDelta:evaluation.chips,multDelta:evaluation.mult}],uniqueSuits=new Set(cards.map(card=>card.si)).size;
      if(handLevel)events.push({phase:'牌型强化',source:`${evaluation.type} Lv.${handLevel}`,detail:`基础筹码 +${handChipBonus}，基础倍率 +${handMultBonus}`,chipsDelta:handChipBonus,multDelta:handMultBonus});
      let chips=baseHandChips,mult=baseHandMult,xmult=1,cardXmultTotal=1,cardBonusChips=0,cardBonusMult=0,faceRuleApplied=false,cardGoldDelta=0,personaGoldDelta=0,pendingPersonaCommit=null,committedResult=null,stageLimitEvent=null;
      for(const card of evaluation.scoringCards){
        const attributes=shopRuntime.cardUpgradeAttributes(card),baseFace=pokerEngine.faceChipValue(card),face=stageLimitRuntime?.adjustFaceChips(stageLimitInstance,card,baseFace)??baseFace,suitBonus=Number(growth.suitChipBonusBySuit?.[card.s]||0),bonus=attributes.bonusChips+suitBonus,cardMult=attributes.bonusMult,cardXmult=attributes.bonusXmultFactor,cardGold=attributes.bonusCoins,details=[];
        chips+=face+bonus;mult+=cardMult;xmult*=cardXmult;cardXmultTotal*=cardXmult;cardBonusChips+=bonus;cardBonusMult+=cardMult;cardGoldDelta+=cardGold;if(face!==baseFace)faceRuleApplied=true;
        details.push(`+${face}${face!==baseFace?'（规则调整）':''}${attributes.bonusChips?`，单卡强化 +${attributes.bonusChips}`:''}${suitBonus?`，花色强化 +${suitBonus}`:''} 筹码`);if(cardMult)details.push(`基础倍率 +${cardMult}`);if(cardXmult!==1)details.push(`独立倍率 ×${Number(cardXmult.toFixed(4))}`);if(cardGold)details.push(`金币 +${cardGold}`);
        events.push({phase:'计分牌',source:`${card.r}${card.s}`,cardUid:card.uid,detail:details.join('，'),chipsDelta:face+bonus,multDelta:cardMult,xmultFactor:cardXmult,coinsDelta:cardGold});
      }
      const handConfig=handTypes.find(item=>item.id===evaluation.typeId),scoringUniqueSuitCount=new Set(evaluation.scoringCards.map(card=>card.s)).size,runtimeContext={runTemplateId:context.runTemplateId,nodeId:context.nodeId,encounterId:context.encounterId,handIndex:context.handIndex,submittedCards:cards,scoringCards:evaluation.scoringCards,scoringCardCount:evaluation.scoringCards.length,scoringUniqueSuitCount,currentHandCardCount:context.currentHandCardCount,handType:evaluation.type,handTypeId:evaluation.typeId,handPriority:handConfig?.priority||0,handQualityId:handConfig?.qualityId||'NORMAL',previousHandType:context.previousHandType,uniqueSuitCount:uniqueSuits,straight:evaluation.straight,flush:evaluation.flush,hasMatchedRankStructure:evaluation.pair,remainingHands:context.remainingHands,remainingDiscards:context.remainingDiscards,disabledSlotIndexes:context.disabledSlotIndexes||[]},runtimeScoreLayers={chips,mult,xmult},runtimeResult=personaRuntime.evaluateHand(runtimeContext,{commit:false,scoreLayers:runtimeScoreLayers}),previewHistory=runtimeResult.state.personaHistory;
      pendingPersonaCommit={runtimeContext,runtimeScoreLayers};
      chips+=runtimeResult.chipsDelta;mult+=runtimeResult.multDelta;xmult*=runtimeResult.finalMultiplier;personaGoldDelta+=runtimeResult.coinsDelta||0;
      const beforeLimit={chips,mult,xmult},limitAdjusted=stageLimitRuntime?.adjustScore(stageLimitInstance,{handIndex:context.handIndex,submittedCount:cards.length,handTypeId:evaluation.typeId,handQualityId:handConfig?.qualityId||'NORMAL',previousHandTypeId:context.previousHandTypeId,baseHandChips,baseHandMult,chips,mult,cardXmult:cardXmultTotal,personaXmult:runtimeResult.finalMultiplier,bonusChips:cardBonusChips+runtimeResult.chipsDelta,bonusMult:cardBonusMult+runtimeResult.multDelta,personaChips:runtimeResult.chipsDelta,personaMult:runtimeResult.multDelta,personaTriggerCount:runtimeResult.logs.filter(item=>item.triggered).length})||beforeLimit;
      if(limitAdjusted.applied){chips=limitAdjusted.chips;mult=limitAdjusted.mult;xmult=limitAdjusted.xmult;stageLimitEvent={phase:'关卡限制',source:stageLimitView?.name||'本场规则',detail:stageLimitView?.description||'规则生效',chipsDelta:chips-beforeLimit.chips,multDelta:mult-beforeLimit.mult,xmultFactor:beforeLimit.xmult?xmult/beforeLimit.xmult:1};}
      for(const log of runtimeResult.logs){
        if(!log.triggered)continue;
        for(const effect of log.effects||[]){
          const echo=effect.repeatIndex?'（完美回响）':'',attributeLabel=effect.attributeSource==='SUB'?`第${effect.attributePosition}属性`:'主属性',eventName=log.displayName||log.name,common={phase:'人格牌',source:effect.attributeSource==='SUB'?`${eventName} · ${attributeLabel}`:eventName,attributeLabel,effectType:effect.type,targetCardUid:effect.targetCardUid||effect.cardUid||null};
          if(effect.type==='ADD_CHIPS')events.push({...common,detail:`+${effect.value} 筹码${echo}`,chipsDelta:effect.value});
          else if(effect.type==='ADD_MULT')events.push({...common,detail:`+${effect.value} 倍率${echo}`,multDelta:effect.value});
          else if(effect.type==='ADD_XMULT_RATE')events.push({...common,detail:`独立倍率 +${Number((effect.value*100).toFixed(4))}%${echo}`,xmultFactor:1+effect.value});
          else if(effect.type==='ADD_COINS')events.push({...common,detail:`+${effect.value} 金币${echo}`,coinsDelta:effect.value});
          else if(effect.type==='MULTIPLY_FINAL')events.push({...common,detail:`最终倍率 ×${effect.value}${echo}`,xmultFactor:effect.value});
          else if(['MODIFY_CARD','ADD_CARD_CHIPS','UPGRADE_CARD'].includes(effect.type))events.push({...common,detail:effect.detail||effect.effectText||`目标卡牌获得强化${echo}`});
        }
      }
      if(faceRuleApplied&&!stageLimitEvent)stageLimitEvent={phase:'关卡限制',source:stageLimitView?.name||'本场规则',detail:stageLimitView?.description||'规则生效'};
      if(stageLimitEvent)events.push(stageLimitEvent);
      chips=clean(chips);mult=clean(mult);xmult=clean(xmult);const total=Math.max(minScore,Math.round(chips*mult*xmult));
      if(consume&&pendingPersonaCommit){
        committedResult=personaRuntime.evaluateHand(pendingPersonaCommit.runtimeContext,{commit:true,scoreLayers:pendingPersonaCommit.runtimeScoreLayers});
        for(const log of committedResult.logs.filter(item=>item.triggered))events.push({phase:'人格调试',source:log.displayName||log.name,detail:`人格层得分 ${committedResult.scoreBefore} → ${committedResult.scoreAfter}；状态 ${JSON.stringify(log.runtimeBefore)} → ${JSON.stringify(log.runtimeAfter)}`});
      }
      events.push({phase:'汇总',source:'最终得分',detail:`${display(chips)} × ${display(mult)}${xmult!==1?` × ${display(xmult)}`:''} = ${total}`});
      const personaLogs=committedResult?.logs||runtimeResult.logs,breakdown=personaFeedback.buildScoreBreakdown({baseLayers:runtimeScoreLayers,personaLogs,otherEvents:stageLimitEvent?[stageLimitEvent]:[],finalLayers:{chips,mult,xmult},finalScore:total});
      return{...evaluation,chips,mult,xmult,goldDelta:cardGoldDelta+personaGoldDelta,cardGoldDelta,personaGoldDelta,total,events,personaLogs,previewHistory,breakdown};
    }

    return Object.freeze({resolve});
  }

  root.BattleScoreRuntime=Object.freeze({create});
})(globalThis);

// SOURCE: game/ai-persona-selection-client.js
+(function(root){
  'use strict';
  const DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS=6500;
  function create({endpoint='',timeoutMs=DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS,fetchImpl=root.fetch,abortController=root.AbortController}={}){
    const normalizedEndpoint=String(endpoint||'').trim();
    function isConfigured(){return /^https?:\/\//i.test(normalizedEndpoint)&&typeof fetchImpl==='function'}
    async function select(request){
      if(!isConfigured())return{ok:false,status:'DISABLED',response:null};
      const controller=typeof abortController==='function'?new abortController():null,timer=controller?setTimeout(()=>controller.abort(),timeoutMs):null;
      try{
        const response=await fetchImpl(normalizedEndpoint,{method:'POST',headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},body:JSON.stringify(request),signal:controller?.signal});
        if(!response.ok)return{ok:false,status:`HTTP_${response.status}`,response:null};
        const body=await response.json();
        return{ok:true,status:'REMOTE_RESPONSE',response:body};
      }catch(error){return{ok:false,status:error?.name==='AbortError'?'TIMEOUT':'NETWORK_ERROR',response:null}}
      finally{if(timer)clearTimeout(timer)}
    }
    return Object.freeze({isConfigured,select});
  }
  root.AiPersonaSelectionClient=Object.freeze({create,DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS});
})(globalThis);


// SOURCE: game/ai-persona-name-client.js
+(function(root){
  'use strict';
  // AI 人格命名客户端：独立提示词与契约（见规格 6.1/6.2）。
  // 服务端只负责出名字；响应由客户端严格校验（恰好五字段、绑定信息一致、名字格式与保留清单），
  // 不信任服务端与模型。保留清单与 Tools/AIProxy 服务端一致，由共享测试向量（PersonaNameValidationCases.json）锁定。
  const NAME_STYLE_VERSION='PERSONA_NAME_STYLE_V1_1';
  const DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS=3000; // 规格：获得窗口最多等待 3 秒
  const BASE_PERSONA_NAMES=[
    '隐境寻路者','断舍离者','善变漫游者','留手谋划者','满手承诺者',
    '终局观察者','克制的赌徒','结构收藏家','花色漫游者','同色共鸣者'
  ];
  const BOSS_BASE_NAMES=[
    '实界之镜','箱中之神','向死之渊','规训之眼','意志之囚',
    '迷宫之骑','无体之器','离情之徒','拒翔之翼','意义守夜者'
  ];
  const BOSS_VARIANT_PREFIXES=['突变的','适应的','强化的','重组的'];
  const HANZI_3_6=/^[一-鿿]{3,6}$/;
  function fullReservedNames(){return[...BASE_PERSONA_NAMES,...BOSS_BASE_NAMES,...BOSS_VARIANT_PREFIXES.flatMap(prefix=>BOSS_BASE_NAMES.map(name=>prefix+name))]}
  function validateDisplayName(value,{reservedNames=[],usedNames=[]}={}){
    if(typeof value!=='string')return{ok:false,reason:'not_string'};
    if(!HANZI_3_6.test(value))return{ok:false,reason:'invalid_format'};
    const reserved=new Set([...reservedNames,...usedNames].filter(item=>typeof item==='string'));
    if(reserved.has(value))return{ok:false,reason:'reserved_name'};
    return{ok:true};
  }
  // 响应校验（规格 6.1）：恰好五个字段，绑定信息必须与请求一致，displayName 必须过格式+保留清单。
  function validateResponse(request,response){
    if(!request||typeof request!=='object'||!response||typeof response!=='object'||Array.isArray(response))return{ok:false,reason:'bad_response'};
    if(JSON.stringify(Object.keys(response).sort())!==JSON.stringify(['displayName','requestId','schemaVersion','selectedCandidateId','styleVersion']))return{ok:false,reason:'bad_response'};
    if(response.schemaVersion!==1||response.styleVersion!==NAME_STYLE_VERSION)return{ok:false,reason:'version_mismatch'};
    if(response.requestId!==request.requestId||response.selectedCandidateId!==request.selectedCandidateId)return{ok:false,reason:'binding_mismatch'};
    const nameCheck=validateDisplayName(response.displayName,{reservedNames:fullReservedNames(),usedNames:request.usedNames||[]});
    if(!nameCheck.ok)return{ok:false,reason:`invalid_display_name:${nameCheck.reason}`};
    return{ok:true,displayName:response.displayName};
  }
  function create({endpoint='',timeoutMs=DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS,fetchImpl=root.fetch,abortController=root.AbortController}={}){
    const normalizedEndpoint=String(endpoint||'').trim();
    function isConfigured(){return /^https?:\/\//i.test(normalizedEndpoint)&&typeof fetchImpl==='function'}
    async function name(request){
      if(!isConfigured())return{ok:false,status:'DISABLED',response:null};
      const controller=typeof abortController==='function'?new abortController():null,timer=controller?setTimeout(()=>controller.abort(),timeoutMs):null;
      try{
        const response=await fetchImpl(normalizedEndpoint,{method:'POST',headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},body:JSON.stringify(request),signal:controller?.signal,timeoutMs});
        if(!response.ok)return{ok:false,status:`HTTP_${response.status}`,response:null};
        const body=await response.json();
        return{ok:true,status:'REMOTE_RESPONSE',response:body};
      }catch(error){return{ok:false,status:error?.name==='AbortError'?'TIMEOUT':'NETWORK_ERROR',response:null}}
      finally{if(timer)clearTimeout(timer)}
    }
    return Object.freeze({isConfigured,name});
  }
  root.AiPersonaNameClient=Object.freeze({create,DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS,NAME_STYLE_VERSION,fullReservedNames,validateDisplayName,validateResponse});
})(globalThis);


// SOURCE: persona/persona-collection.js
(function(root){
  'use strict';
  const STORAGE_KEY='persona-permanent-collection-v1';
  const VERSION=1;
  const clone=value=>value==null?value:JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(value));
  const safeId=value=>String(value||'persona').replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9_-]/g,'_');

  function initialRecord(template){
    return{collectionId:`INITIAL_${template.id}`,cardId:template.id,templateId:template.id,source:'INITIAL_COLLECTION',templateSnapshot:null,acquiredAt:null,originalInstanceId:null,runsSinceLastUsed:0,version:1};
  }
  function validRecord(record){
    return !!record&&record.version===1&&typeof record.collectionId==='string'&&!!record.collectionId&&typeof record.cardId==='string'&&!!record.cardId&&typeof record.templateId==='string'&&!!record.templateId&&record.source==='CARRY_OUT'&&record.templateSnapshot?.id===record.templateId&&!('subAffixSlots' in record)&&!('runtimeState' in record);
  }
  function create({storage=root.localStorage,initialTemplates=[],now=()=>Date.now()}={}){
    const initial=(initialTemplates||[]).filter(template=>template?.id).map(initialRecord);
    let cached=null;
    function empty(){return{version:VERSION,records:[]}}
    function read(){
      if(cached)return clone(cached);
      try{const parsed=JSON.parse(storage?.getItem?.(STORAGE_KEY)||'null');cached=parsed?.version===VERSION&&Array.isArray(parsed.records)&&parsed.records.every(validRecord)?parsed:empty()}catch{cached=empty()}
      return clone(cached);
    }
    function persist(state){
      cached=clone(state);
      try{storage?.setItem?.(STORAGE_KEY,JSON.stringify(cached));return{ok:true}}catch(error){return{ok:false,error}}
    }
    function list(){return clone([...initial,...read().records.map(record=>({...record,runsSinceLastUsed:Number.isInteger(record.runsSinceLastUsed)?record.runsSinceLastUsed:0}))])}
    function get(cardId){return list().find(record=>record.cardId===cardId)||null}
    function carryOut({instance,template,runTemplateId=null,nodeId=null}={}){
      if(!instance?.instanceId||!template?.id||instance.templateId!==template.id)return{ok:false,reason:'INVALID_PERSONA'};
      const state=read(),existing=state.records.find(record=>record.originalInstanceId===instance.instanceId);
      if(existing)return{ok:true,duplicate:true,record:clone(existing)};
      const collectionId=`CARRY_${safeId(instance.instanceId)}`,record={collectionId,cardId:collectionId,templateId:template.id,source:'CARRY_OUT',templateSnapshot:clone(template),acquiredAt:now(),originalInstanceId:instance.instanceId,acquiredRunTemplateId:runTemplateId,acquiredAtNodeId:nodeId,runsSinceLastUsed:0,version:1};
      const result=persist({...state,records:[...state.records,record]});
      return result.ok?{ok:true,duplicate:false,record:clone(record)}:{ok:false,reason:'STORAGE_FAILED',error:result.error};
    }
    function beginRun(usedCardIds=[]){
      const used=new Set(usedCardIds||[]),state=read();
      state.records=state.records.map(record=>({...record,runsSinceLastUsed:used.has(record.cardId)?0:Math.max(0,Number(record.runsSinceLastUsed||0))+1}));
      const result=persist(state);return result.ok?{ok:true,records:list()}:{ok:false,reason:'STORAGE_FAILED',error:result.error};
    }
    function backfillPortraits(){
      // 历史收藏记录的 AI 人格可能没有配图：按内部分类随机补一张并写回收藏存储（幂等，仅补缺失项）。
      // 宿主在模块加载后才注入 localStorage（NativeRestoreStorage），模块加载期首读把缓存钉在了空集合上，
      // 必须先失效缓存重读存储，否则旧收藏记录永远不可见（甚至会被之后的 carryOut 覆盖丢失）。
      cached=null;
      const pool=root.AiPersonaPortraitPool;if(!pool)return{ok:false,reason:'POOL_UNAVAILABLE'};
      const state=read();let changed=false;
      for(const record of state.records){const snapshot=record.templateSnapshot;
        if(snapshot?.aiPersonaMeta?.internalDirectionId&&!snapshot.portrait){const portrait=pool.randomPortraitFor(snapshot.aiPersonaMeta.internalDirectionId);if(portrait){snapshot.portrait=portrait;changed=true}}}
      if(!changed)return{ok:true,changed:false};
      const result=persist(state);return result.ok?{ok:true,changed:true}:{ok:false,reason:'STORAGE_FAILED',error:result.error};
    }
    return{list,get,carryOut,beginRun,backfillPortraits,storageKey:STORAGE_KEY,version:VERSION};
  }
  root.PersonaCollection={create,STORAGE_KEY,VERSION};
})(globalThis);

function renderScoreBreakdown(breakdown){const panel=$('#score-breakdown'),number=uiFormatters.formatBattleNumber;if(!breakdown){panel.classList.add('hidden');panel.innerHTML='';return}const signed=value=>`${value>0?'+':''}${number(value)}`,personaItems=breakdown.personas.map(item=>{const changes=[item.chipsDelta?`筹码 ${signed(item.chipsDelta)}`:'',item.multDelta?`倍率 ${signed(item.multDelta)}`:'',item.finalMultiplier!==1?`最终倍率 ×${number(item.finalMultiplier)}`:''].filter(Boolean).join('、');return`<li><b>${escapePersonaUiText(item.displayName||item.name)}</b> ${changes}</li>`}),otherChanges=[breakdown.other.chipsDelta?`筹码 ${signed(breakdown.other.chipsDelta)}`:'',breakdown.other.multDelta?`倍率 ${signed(breakdown.other.multDelta)}`:'',breakdown.other.finalMultiplier!==1?`最终倍率 ×${number(breakdown.other.finalMultiplier)}`:''].filter(Boolean).join('、');panel.innerHTML=`<details><summary>查看本手得分详情 <b>${Math.round(breakdown.finalScore)} 分</b></summary><ol><li>基础 ${number(breakdown.base.chips)} 筹码 × ${number(breakdown.base.mult)} 倍率</li>${personaItems.join('')}${otherChanges?`<li><b>其他修正</b> ${otherChanges}</li>`:''}<li>结算 ${number(breakdown.final.chips)} × ${number(breakdown.final.mult)}${breakdown.final.xmult!==1?` × ${number(breakdown.final.xmult)}`:''}</li></ol></details>`;panel.classList.remove('hidden')}

function updateTargetMetrics(mutator){if(!isTargetRun())return;const metrics=globalThis.runController.getRunMetrics();mutator(metrics);globalThis.runController.updateRunMetrics(metrics)}
function targetRawEvent(type,payload={}){updateTargetMetrics(metrics=>metrics.rawEvents.push({type,runTemplateId:currentRunTemplate()?.id,nodeId:currentStageNode()?.id||null,battleIndexCompat:battleIndex,encounterId:currentEncounterDefinition()?.id||null,timestamp:Date.now(),...payload}))}
function recordBattleStart(){const node=currentStageNode(),encounter=currentEncounterDefinition(),target=battleTarget();behaviorAnalytics.recordBattleStart(behavior,{index:battleIndex,nodeId:node?.id||null,encounterId:encounter?.id||null,target,startScore:score,startingHands:hands,startingDiscards:discards});targetRawEvent('BATTLE_START',{targetScore:target,startingHands:hands,startingDiscards:discards})}
function recordPlay(cards,result){
  behaviorAnalytics.recordPlay(behavior,{cards,result,battleIndex,nodeId:currentStageNode()?.id||null,encounterId:currentEncounterDefinition()?.id||null,currentHandCardCount:hand.length,handIndex:battlePlayed+1,remainingHands:hands,remainingDiscards:discards});
  updateTargetMetrics(metrics=>{metrics.runTotalScore+=result.total;metrics.maxSingleHandScore=Math.max(metrics.maxSingleHandScore,result.total);metrics.handsPlayedByNode[currentStageNode().id]=(metrics.handsPlayedByNode[currentStageNode().id]||0)+1;for(const log of result.personaLogs||[]){if(!log.triggered)continue;metrics.personaTriggersByInstance[log.instanceId]=(metrics.personaTriggersByInstance[log.instanceId]||0)+1}const triggered=(result.personaLogs||[]).filter(log=>log.triggered);metrics.rawEvents.push({type:'PLAY',runTemplateId:currentRunTemplate().id,nodeId:currentStageNode().id,battleIndexCompat:battleIndex,encounterId:currentEncounterDefinition()?.id||null,handIndex:battlePlayed+1,handType:result.type,handTypeId:result.typeId,submittedCardCount:cards.length,scoringCardCount:result.scoringCards.length,score:result.total,personaTriggerCount:triggered.length,discardAction:false,discardedCardCount:0,personaInstanceIds:triggered.map(log=>log.instanceId),timestamp:Date.now()})});
}
function recordDiscard(count){behaviorAnalytics.recordDiscard(behavior,{count,battleIndex,nodeId:currentStageNode()?.id||null,encounterId:currentEncounterDefinition()?.id||null});updateTargetMetrics(metrics=>{metrics.discardsUsedByNode[currentStageNode().id]=(metrics.discardsUsedByNode[currentStageNode().id]||0)+1});targetRawEvent('DISCARD',{handIndex:battlePlayed+1,handType:null,submittedCardCount:0,scoringCardCount:0,score:0,personaTriggerCount:0,discardAction:true,discardedCardCount:count,remainingDiscards:discards-1})}
function recordBattleEnd(win){behaviorAnalytics.recordBattleEnd(behavior,{score,remainingHands:hands,remainingDiscards:discards,win});updateTargetMetrics(metrics=>{const nodeId=currentStageNode().id,entry={nodeId,battleIndexCompat:battleIndex,score,targetScore:battleTarget(),win,remainingHands:hands,remainingDiscards:discards};metrics.battleScores=metrics.battleScores.filter(item=>item.nodeId!==nodeId).concat(entry);metrics.battleTargetScores=metrics.battleTargetScores.filter(item=>item.nodeId!==nodeId).concat({nodeId,targetScore:battleTarget()});metrics.battleDurationMs[nodeId]=Math.max(0,Date.now()-(globalThis.runController.getCurrentRuntime()?.enteredAt||Date.now()));metrics.rawEvents.push({type:'BATTLE_END',...entry,timestamp:Date.now()})})}

}
function aiPersonaGenerationActive(){const state=globalThis.runController?.getState();return isTargetRun()&&(state?.personaGenerationMode==='LOCAL_V1'||state?.developerOptions?.offlineAiPersonaEnabled===true)}
function aiPersonaSequenceNumber(){
  const names=[...(personaCollection.list()||[]).map(record=>record.templateSnapshot?.name),...Object.values(personaRuntime.getState()?.dynamicPersonaTemplatesById||{}).map(template=>template.name)],numbers=names.map(name=>String(name||'').match(/^AI人格(\d+)$/)).filter(Boolean).map(match=>Number(match[1]));return Math.max(0,...numbers)+1;
}
function aiPersonaReferences(){
  const state=personaRuntime.getState(),templates=new Map((runManifest.personaTemplates?.templates||[]).map(template=>[template.id,template]));for(const [id,template] of Object.entries(state.dynamicPersonaTemplatesById||{}))templates.set(id,template);return aiPersonaSimilarity.collectReferences({personaState:state,templatesById:templates,permanentRecords:personaCollection.list(),discardedTemplates:[]});
}
function commitAiPersonaSelection(node,profile,assigned,preparation,response,transportStatus){
  const result=aiPersonaGenerationService.finalize(preparation,response);
  if(!result.ok)return null;if(result.kind==='GENERATED_PERSONA')personaRuntime.registerTemplate(result.template);const templateId=result.kind==='GENERATED_PERSONA'?result.template.id:result.templateId,state=globalThis.runController.getState(),remote=result.selectionEnvelope?.responseAccepted===true,source=state.developerOptions?.offlineAiPersonaEnabled===true?'AI_GENERATED_OFFLINE_DEV':remote?'AI_GENERATED_REMOTE_V1':'AI_GENERATED_LOCAL_V1',instance=personaRuntime.createInstance(templateId,{source,generatedAtNodeId:node.id,generatedAtBattleIndexCompat:state.battleIndexCompat});
  globalThis.runController.setNodeRuntime({generatedPersonaInstanceId:instance.instanceId,aiPersonaGeneration:{schemaVersion:1,kind:result.kind,directionId:assigned.directionId,selectionSource:result.selectionSource||'LIBRARY_FALLBACK',transportStatus,selectedCandidateId:result.selectedCandidateId||null,templateId,mechanismFingerprint:result.template?.aiPersonaMeta?.mechanismFingerprint||null,trace:result.trace||null}});return instance;
}
// ---------- AI 命名（规格 6.1/6.2）：人格定型后请求显示名，获得窗口最多等待 3 秒 ----------
function aiPersonaNameRequestId(nodeId){return `AI_PERSONA_NAME_V1:${nodeId}:${Date.now().toString(36)}${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,8)}`}
function usedAiPersonaNames(state){const names=[];for(const template of Object.values(state?.dynamicPersonaTemplatesById||{})){const display=template?.aiPersonaMeta?.naming?.displayName;if(typeof display==='string'&&display.trim())names.push(display)}return names.slice(-2)}
async function requestAiPersonaName(instance,{node,preparation,directionId}){
  if(!instance||!aiPersonaNameClient.isConfigured())return instance;
  const template=personaRuntime.getTemplate(instance.templateId),meta=template?.aiPersonaMeta;
  if(!meta?.playerCopy||!meta?.sourceCandidateId)return instance; // 仅 AI 生成人格参与命名（库兜底人格不命名）
  const request={schemaVersion:1,requestId:aiPersonaNameRequestId(node.id),selectionRequestId:preparation.request.requestId,runtimeNodeId:node.id,selectedCandidateId:meta.sourceCandidateId,directionId,styleVersion:globalThis.AiPersonaNameClient.NAME_STYLE_VERSION,playerCopy:meta.playerCopy,usedNames:usedAiPersonaNames(personaRuntime.getState())};
  const naming={schemaVersion:1,styleVersion:globalThis.AiPersonaNameClient.NAME_STYLE_VERSION,status:'FALLBACK',displayName:null};
  const result=await aiPersonaNameClient.name(request);
  if(result.ok&&result.response){const checked=globalThis.AiPersonaNameClient.validateResponse(request,result.response);if(checked.ok){naming.status='AI_NAMED';naming.displayName=checked.displayName}}
  try{const current=personaRuntime.getTemplate(instance.templateId);if(current)personaRuntime.registerTemplate({...current,aiPersonaMeta:{...current.aiPersonaMeta,naming}})}catch(error){console.warn('AI persona naming write failed:',error)}
  return instance;
}
function generateAiPersonaV1(node,profile,snapshot){
  const assigned=aiPersonaGenerationService.assignDirection({runtimeNodeId:node.id,assignments:globalThis.runController.getAiPersonaDirectionAssignments(),randomValue:Math.random()});globalThis.runController.setAiPersonaDirectionAssignments(assigned.assignments);
  const fallbackTemplate=personaRuntime.getTemplate(profile.templateId),preparation=aiPersonaGenerationService.prepare({snapshot,directionId:assigned.directionId,handTypes:currentHandTypes(),references:aiPersonaReferences(),sequenceNumber:aiPersonaSequenceNumber(),portrait:fallbackTemplate?.portrait||null,fallbackTemplates:fallbackTemplate?[fallbackTemplate]:[]});
  const finish=instance=>requestAiPersonaName(instance,{node,preparation,directionId:assigned.directionId});
  if(!aiPersonaSelectionClient.isConfigured()){const instance=commitAiPersonaSelection(node,profile,assigned,preparation,null,'DISABLED');return finish(instance)}
  return aiPersonaSelectionClient.select(preparation.request).then(selection=>finish(commitAiPersonaSelection(node,profile,assigned,preparation,selection.response,selection.status)));
}

function openDeveloperTools(){
  if(!developerModeActive()||currentStageNode()?.type!=='BATTLE')return;
  const dialog=$('#developer-tools-dialog'),state=globalThis.runController?.getState(),active=aiPersonaGenerationActive();
  const mode=active?(aiPersonaSelectionClient.isConfigured()?'本地合法候选 + 远程 AI 选择':'本地安全生成'):'旧原型兼容模式';
  const history=Object.entries(state?.nodeRuntimeById||{}).flatMap(([nodeId,runtime])=>{
    const result=runtime.data?.aiPersonaGeneration;if(!result)return[];
    return[`${nodeId}：${result.transportStatus||'未记录'} / ${result.selectionSource||'未记录'}`];
  });
  $('#developer-ai-status').textContent=[`本局人格生成：${mode}`,...history].join('；');
  if(!dialog.open)dialog.showModal();window.gameSfx?.('button');
}

function createRunDeck(){return globalThis.PokerEngine.createStandardDeck()}
function createFreshBattleDeck(){return shuffle(runDeck.map((card,index)=>({...card,uid:`battle-${battleIndex}-${index}-${Date.now()}`})))}
function sortHandCards(){
  const selectedUids=new Set([...selected].map(index=>hand[index]?.uid).filter(Boolean)),suitOrder={'♥':0,'♦':1,'♣':2,'♠':3};
  hand.sort((a,b)=>handSortMode==='suit'?(suitOrder[a.s]-suitOrder[b.s]||b.ri-a.ri):(b.ri-a.ri||suitOrder[a.s]-suitOrder[b.s]));
  selected=new Set(hand.map((card,index)=>selectedUids.has(card.uid)?index:-1).filter(index=>index>=0));
}
function setHandSort(mode){if(inputLocked||!['rank','suit'].includes(mode))return;handSortMode=mode;sortHandCards();render();window.gameSfx?.('button')}
function setupBattleDeck(){renderScoreBreakdown(null);showPersonaTriggerFeedback();deck=createFreshBattleDeck();hand=[];selected.clear();currentPlayed=[];usedCards=[];discardedCards=[];usedCount=0;drawTo(battleHandSize());sortHandCards()}
function buildRunSaveState(){
  const selectedUids=[...selected].map(index=>hand[index]?.uid).filter(Boolean);
  return{runState:globalThis.runController?.serializeState()||null,runDeck,deck,hand,currentPlayed,usedCards,discardedCards,selectedUids,usedCount,score,hands,discards,played,suitPlays,pairs,battleIndex,battlePlayed,coins,earnedThisBattle,cardGoldThisBattle,personaGoldThisBattle,extraDiscard,extraHand,nextTargetModifier,currentEncounter,lastHandType,lastHandTypeId,behavior,currentPersonaReport,handSortMode,equippedPersonaIds,settlementWasWin,forgeStep,forgeResults,acquiredPersona,choiceSelectedIndex,currentRouteNodes,currentRandomEvent};
}
function restoreRunSaveState(save){
  const state=save?.state;if(!state)return false;
  runDeck=state.runDeck.map(card=>({...card}));deck=state.deck.map(card=>({...card}));hand=state.hand.map(card=>({...card}));currentPlayed=(state.currentPlayed||[]).map(card=>({...card}));usedCards=(state.usedCards||[]).map(card=>({...card}));discardedCards=(state.discardedCards||[]).map(card=>({...card}));
  usedCount=state.usedCount||0;score=state.score||0;hands=state.hands??battleConfig.baseHands;discards=state.discards??battleConfig.baseDiscards;played=state.played||0;suitPlays=state.suitPlays||0;pairs=state.pairs||0;battleIndex=state.battleIndex||0;battlePlayed=state.battlePlayed||0;coins=state.coins||0;earnedThisBattle=state.earnedThisBattle||0;cardGoldThisBattle=state.cardGoldThisBattle||0;personaGoldThisBattle=state.personaGoldThisBattle||0;extraDiscard=state.extraDiscard||0;extraHand=state.extraHand||0;nextTargetModifier=state.nextTargetModifier||1;
  currentEncounter=state.currentEncounter||null;lastHandType=state.lastHandType||null;lastHandTypeId=state.lastHandTypeId||currentHandTypes().find(type=>type.name===lastHandType)?.id||null;behavior=state.behavior||createBehaviorAggregate();if(!Array.isArray(behavior.discards))behavior.discards=[];currentPersonaReport=state.currentPersonaReport||null;handSortMode=['rank','suit'].includes(state.handSortMode)?state.handSortMode:'rank';equippedPersonaIds=normalizedGalleryLoadout(state.equippedPersonaIds||[]);settlementWasWin=!!state.settlementWasWin;forgeStep=state.forgeStep||0;forgeResults=state.forgeResults||[];acquiredPersona=state.acquiredPersona||null;choiceSelectedIndex=state.choiceSelectedIndex||0;currentRouteNodes=state.currentRouteNodes||[];currentRandomEvent=state.currentRandomEvent||null;selectedShopItemId=null;deckShopItemId=null;
  selected=new Set((state.selectedUids||[]).map(uid=>hand.findIndex(card=>card.uid===uid)).filter(index=>index>=0));inputLocked=false;animatePlayed=false;
  render();
  try{globalThis.runController.restoreRun(state.runState,{phase:save.phase});renderEncounter();const migrationCredit=personaRuntime.consumeMigrationCoinCredit();if(migrationCredit){coins+=migrationCredit;render()}return true}catch(error){console.warn('Run restore rejected:',error);return false}
}
window.buildRunSaveState=buildRunSaveState;window.restoreRunSaveState=restoreRunSaveState;
function reset(){runDeck=createRunDeck();battleIndex=0;battlePlayed=0;coins=0;earnedThisBattle=0;cardGoldThisBattle=0;personaGoldThisBattle=0;extraDiscard=0;extraHand=0;nextTargetModifier=1;currentRouteNodes=[];currentRandomEvent=null;selectedShopItemId=null;selectedShopPersonaId=null;deckShopItemId=null;shopTab='goods';score=0;hands=battleConfig.baseHands;discards=battleConfig.baseDiscards;played=0;suitPlays=0;pairs=0;inputLocked=false;currentEncounter=null;lastHandType=null;lastHandTypeId=null;behavior=createBehaviorAggregate();currentPersonaReport=null;renderScoreBreakdown(null);showPersonaTriggerFeedback();setupBattleDeck();render()}
function drawTo(n){while(hand.length<n&&deck.length)hand.push(deck.pop())}
function toggle(i){if(inputLocked)return;const limit=currentEncounter?.maxSelection||currentRunTemplate()?.actionRules?.maxSelection||battleConfig.maxSelection;selected.has(i)?selected.delete(i):selected.size<limit&&selected.add(i);render()}
function evaluate(cards){return globalThis.PokerEngine.evaluate(cards,currentHandTypes(),battleConfig.maxSelection)}
function cardArtPath(card){return cardPresentation.artPath(card)}
function cardArtClass(card){return cardPresentation.artClass(card)}
function cardArtImage(card,className='card-art-image'){return cardPresentation.artImage(card,className)}
function cardFace(card){return cardPresentation.faceMarkup(card)}
function renderPlayedCards(){
  const row=$('#played-cards'),slots=new Array(battleConfig.maxSelection).fill(null),start=Math.floor((battleConfig.maxSelection-currentPlayed.length)/2);currentPlayed.forEach((card,index)=>slots[start+index]=card);
  row.innerHTML=slots.map(card=>card?`<div class="card playing-card played-card ${cardArtClass(card)} ${animatePlayed?'':'settled'} ${card.c}" data-uid="${escapePersonaUiText(card.uid)}" aria-label="已打出 ${card.r}${card.s}">${cardFace(card)}</div>`:`<div class="enemy-card empty-slot" aria-hidden="true"><span>✦</span><i>P</i></div>`).join('');animatePlayed=false;
}
function renderTurnResource(selector,remaining,maximum,label){
  const resource=$(selector);if(!resource)return;
  const current=Math.max(0,Number(remaining)||0),total=Math.max(0,Number(maximum)||0);
  resource.dataset.remaining=String(current);resource.style?.setProperty?.('--turn-fill',`${total?Math.min(100,current/total*100):0}%`);resource.classList.toggle('is-low',current===1&&total>1);resource.classList.toggle('is-empty',current===0);resource.setAttribute('aria-label',`${label}剩余 ${current} 次，共 ${total} 次`);
}
function render(){
  const target=battleTarget(),handsMaximum=currentEncounter?.startingHands??hands,discardsMaximum=currentEncounter?.startingDiscards??discards,scorePercent=Math.min(100,score/target*100),scoreProgress=$('#score-progress');$('#score').textContent=score;$('#target').textContent=target;$('#hands').textContent=hands;$('#discards').textContent=discards;$('#hands-max').textContent=handsMaximum;$('#discards-max').textContent=discardsMaximum;renderTurnResource('#hands-resource',hands,handsMaximum,'出牌次数');renderTurnResource('#discards-resource',discards,discardsMaximum,'弃牌次数');$('#earned-coins').textContent=earnedThisBattle;$('#current-coins').textContent=coins;$('#deck-count').textContent=deck.length;$('#used-count').textContent=usedCount;$('#progress').style.width=scorePercent+'%';scoreProgress?.setAttribute('aria-valuenow',String(Math.min(score,target)));scoreProgress?.setAttribute('aria-valuemax',String(target));
  const runtimeReady=globalThis.runController?.getState()&&globalThis.runController.getPersonaRuntimeState()?.runPersonaPool?.length,equipped=runtimeReady?personaRuntime.getEquippedPersonas().map(runtimePersonaView):equippedPersonaIds.map(id=>personaPool.find(p=>p.id===id)).filter(Boolean),chipValue=equipped.find(persona=>(persona.template?.effects||[]).some(effect=>effect.type==='ADD_CHIPS')||normalizedPersonaEffectType(persona.effectType)==='ADD_CHIPS')?.template?.effects.find(effect=>effect.type==='ADD_CHIPS')?.value??equipped.find(persona=>normalizedPersonaEffectType(persona.effectType)==='ADD_CHIPS')?.value??0,multValue=equipped.find(persona=>(persona.template?.effects||[]).some(effect=>effect.type==='ADD_MULT')||normalizedPersonaEffectType(persona.effectType)==='ADD_MULT')?.template?.effects.find(effect=>effect.type==='ADD_MULT')?.value??equipped.find(persona=>normalizedPersonaEffectType(persona.effectType)==='ADD_MULT')?.value??0;$('#persona-chip').textContent=`+${chipValue}`;$('#persona-mult').textContent=`+${multValue}`;$('#personas').innerHTML=equipped.map(p=>`<article class="persona has-sub-attributes"><div class="portrait ${p.portrait?'has-art':''}" style="--tone:${p.tone};${p.portrait?`--portrait:url('${p.portrait}')`:''}"><span></span></div><div class="persona-copy"><div class="persona-card-heading"><h3>${escapePersonaUiText(p.name)}</h3></div><p class="persona-battle-trigger">${escapePersonaUiText(p.trigger||p.template?.mainEffect?.triggerText||'条件成立时')}</p><p class="persona-battle-summary"><b>主属性</b><strong>${escapePersonaUiText(p.effect||personaBattleEffectMarkup(p).replace(/<[^>]+>/g,''))}</strong></p>${personaBattleAffixMarkup(p)}</div></article>`).join('');
  renderPlayedCards();
  $('#cards').innerHTML='';hand.forEach((card,i)=>{const d=document.createElement('button');d.className=`card playing-card ${cardArtClass(card)} ${card.c} ${selected.has(i)?'selected':''}`;d.dataset.uid=card.uid;d.setAttribute('aria-label',`${card.r}${card.s}`);d.innerHTML=cardFace(card);d.onclick=()=>toggle(i);$('#cards').append(d)});$('#cards').classList.toggle('flow-locked',inputLocked);
  $('#sort-by-rank').classList.toggle('active',handSortMode==='rank');$('#sort-by-rank').setAttribute('aria-pressed',handSortMode==='rank');$('#sort-by-suit').classList.toggle('active',handSortMode==='suit');$('#sort-by-suit').setAttribute('aria-pressed',handSortMode==='suit');$('#sort-by-rank').disabled=inputLocked;$('#sort-by-suit').disabled=inputLocked;
  $('#play-btn').disabled=inputLocked||!selected.size||!hands;$('#discard-btn').disabled=inputLocked||!selected.size||!discards;updatePreview();
}
function updatePreview(){const cards=[...selected].map(i=>hand[i]);if(!cards.length){$('#preview-type').textContent='等待选择';$('#preview-chips').textContent='—';$('#preview-mult').textContent='×—';$('#preview-score').textContent='—';renderPersonaTriggerPreview();return}renderScoreBreakdown(null);const result=resolveScore(cards,false),number=uiFormatters.formatBattleNumber,feedback=globalThis.PersonaFeedback.feedbackFromLogs(result.personaLogs,{...personaFeedbackContext(),history:result.previewHistory,handTypeId:result.typeId,handTypeName:result.type,kind:'preview'});$('#preview-type').textContent=result.type;$('#preview-chips').textContent=number(result.chips);$('#preview-mult').textContent=`×${number(result.mult)}${result.xmult!==1?` ×${number(result.xmult)}`:''}`;$('#preview-score').textContent=Math.round(result.total);renderPersonaTriggerPreview(feedback)}
function removeSelected(){hand=hand.filter((_,i)=>!selected.has(i));selected.clear()}
const wait=ms=>new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(resolve,ms));
function replayClass(element,className,duration=420){
  if(!element||document.documentElement.classList.contains('reduce-motion'))return;
  element.classList.remove(className);void element.offsetWidth;element.classList.add(className);
  setTimeout(()=>element.classList.remove(className),duration);
}
function showBattleFloat(text,kind='score'){
  if(document.documentElement.classList.contains('reduce-motion'))return;
  const stage=document.querySelector('.battle-stage'),float=document.createElement('div');
  float.className=`battle-float ${kind}`;float.textContent=text;stage.append(float);
  float.addEventListener('animationend',()=>float.remove(),{once:true});
}
function personaEventTarget(event){return event.chipsDelta?'#preview-chips':event.multDelta?'#preview-mult':event.xmultFactor?'#preview-score':'#preview-score'}
function personaSourceElement(name){const index=personaRuntime.getEquippedPersonas().findIndex(instance=>personaRuntime.getTemplate(instance.templateId)?.name===name);return index>=0?document.querySelectorAll('.persona')[index]:null}
function personaCardTarget(event){const uid=event.targetCardUid||event.cardUid;return uid?[...document.querySelectorAll('#played-cards .played-card')].find(card=>card.dataset.uid===String(uid))||null:null}
function personaVisualProfile(event){if(event.xmultFactor)return{kind:'final',outer:'#8d342f',body:'#d9674e',core:'#ffd6bd',tip:'rgba(255,239,222,.98)',halo:'rgba(190,67,48,.62)'};if(event.multDelta)return{kind:'mult',outer:'#5b3c91',body:'#8d6bd3',core:'#d6c1ff',tip:'rgba(244,237,255,.98)',halo:'rgba(124,84,211,.62)'};return{kind:'chips',outer:'#6f3f91',body:'#b57a31',core:'#f2c66b',tip:'rgba(255,244,194,.98)',halo:'rgba(232,181,79,.62)'}}
function detectedPersonaFlowQuality(){const nav=globalThis.navigator||{},cores=nav.hardwareConcurrency||8,memory=nav.deviceMemory||8,base=cores<=4||memory<=4?.58:cores<=6?.78:1;return Math.max(.45,Math.min(1,base*personaFlowQualityScale))}
function bezierPoint(start,control1,control2,end,t){const mt=1-t;return{x:mt**3*start.x+3*mt**2*t*control1.x+3*mt*t**2*control2.x+t**3*end.x,y:mt**3*start.y+3*mt**2*t*control1.y+3*mt*t**2*control2.y+t**3*end.y}}
function bezierTangent(start,control1,control2,end,t){const mt=1-t;return{x:3*mt**2*(control1.x-start.x)+6*mt*t*(control2.x-control1.x)+3*t**2*(end.x-control2.x),y:3*mt**2*(control1.y-start.y)+6*mt*t*(control2.y-control1.y)+3*t**2*(end.y-control2.y)}}
function fluidPathPoint(start,control1,control2,end,t,offset=0,wave=0){const point=bezierPoint(start,control1,control2,end,t),tangent=bezierTangent(start,control1,control2,end,t),length=Math.hypot(tangent.x,tangent.y)||1,shift=offset+Math.sin(t*Math.PI*5)*wave;return{x:point.x-tangent.y/length*shift,y:point.y+tangent.x/length*shift}}
function drawFluidStrand(context,path,tail,head,{color,width,offset=0,wave=0,blur=0,alpha=1,quality=1}){if(head<=tail)return;const steps=Math.max(12,Math.round(34*quality));context.save();context.lineCap='round';context.lineJoin='round';context.strokeStyle=color;context.shadowColor=color;context.shadowBlur=blur*quality;context.globalAlpha=alpha;for(let index=0;index<steps;index++){const a=tail+(head-tail)*index/steps,b=tail+(head-tail)*(index+1)/steps,progress=(index+1)/steps,p1=fluidPathPoint(...path,a,offset,wave),p2=fluidPathPoint(...path,b,offset,wave);context.lineWidth=Math.max(.35,width*Math.sin(Math.PI*progress)*(.58+.42*progress));context.beginPath();context.moveTo(p1.x,p1.y);context.lineTo(p2.x,p2.y);context.stroke()}context.restore()}
function configuredResolutionSpeed(){const speed=Number(window.personaResolutionSpeed);return speed===2?2:speed===1.5?1.5:1}
function beginResolutionPlayback(){resolutionPlaybackRate=configuredResolutionSpeed();document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--resolution-duration-scale',String(1/resolutionPlaybackRate));document.documentElement.classList.add('resolution-playing');return resolutionPlaybackRate}
function endResolutionPlayback(){resolutionAnimating=false;resolutionPlaybackRate=1;document.documentElement.classList.remove('resolution-playing');document.documentElement.style.removeProperty('--resolution-duration-scale')}
function resolutionAccelerate(){return false}
async function waitResolution(ms){let remaining=ms,last=performance.now();while(remaining>0){await wait(Math.min(32,Math.max(1,remaining/resolutionPlaybackRate)));const now=performance.now();remaining-=(now-last)*resolutionPlaybackRate;last=now}}
async function animatePersonaFluid(event,chain={index:0,total:1}){
  const source=personaSourceElement(event.source),target=$(personaEventTarget(event)),cardTarget=personaCardTarget(event),profile=personaVisualProfile(event),reduced=document.documentElement.classList.contains('reduce-motion');
  if(!source||!target||reduced){pulseResolutionEvent(event);return}
  source.classList.add('persona-charging');$('#message').innerHTML=`<span></span>${event.source}正在发动 · ${event.detail}`;window.gameSfx?.('personaCharge');await waitResolution(300);window.gameSfx?.('personaTravel');
  const canvas=document.createElement('canvas'),context=canvas.getContext('2d'),quality=detectedPersonaFlowQuality(),pixelRatio=Math.max(.75,Math.min(1.7,(window.devicePixelRatio||1)*quality));canvas.className='persona-flow-canvas';document.body.append(canvas);let viewportWidth=0,viewportHeight=0,frameCount=0;
  function resizeCanvas(){if(viewportWidth===innerWidth&&viewportHeight===innerHeight)return;viewportWidth=innerWidth;viewportHeight=innerHeight;canvas.width=Math.round(viewportWidth*pixelRatio);canvas.height=Math.round(viewportHeight*pixelRatio);canvas.style.width=`${viewportWidth}px`;canvas.style.height=`${viewportHeight}px`;context.setTransform(pixelRatio,0,0,pixelRatio,0,0)}
  function center(rect,side='center'){return{x:side==='right'?rect.right-2:rect.left+rect.width*.5,y:rect.top+rect.height*.52}}
  function curvedPath(from,to,bend=1){const span=Math.max(100,Math.abs(to.x-from.x));return[from,{x:from.x+span*.3,y:from.y-18*bend},{x:to.x-span*.25,y:to.y+14*bend},to]}
  function liveRoute(progress){const start=center(source.getBoundingClientRect(),'right'),end=center(target.getBoundingClientRect());if(!cardTarget)return{path:curvedPath(start,end),start,end,local:progress};const card=center(cardTarget.getBoundingClientRect()),first=progress<.54;return{path:first?curvedPath(start,card,-1):curvedPath(card,end,1),start:first?start:card,end:first?card:end,local:first?progress/.54:(progress-.54)/.46,card,first}}
  resizeCanvas();const targetRect=target.getBoundingClientRect(),targetPoint=center(targetRect),callout=document.createElement('div');callout.className='persona-effect-callout';callout.dataset.flowKind=profile.kind;callout.style.left=`${Math.min(innerWidth-130,Math.max(130,targetPoint.x))}px`;callout.style.top=`${Math.max(38,targetRect.top-20)}px`;callout.innerHTML=`<b>${escapePersonaUiText(event.source)}</b><span>${escapePersonaUiText(event.detail)}</span>${chain.total>1?`<em>连锁 ${chain.index+1}/${chain.total}</em>`:''}`;document.body.append(callout);
  const duration=760;let elapsed=0,lastFrame=performance.now();await new Promise(resolve=>{function frame(now){resizeCanvas();elapsed+=(now-lastFrame)*resolutionPlaybackRate;lastFrame=now;const progress=Math.min(1,elapsed/duration),route=liveRoute(progress),local=Math.min(1,route.local),head=1-Math.pow(1-local,2.1),tail=Math.max(0,head-.31-local*.06),path=route.path,power=1+chain.index*.1;context.clearRect(0,0,viewportWidth,viewportHeight);context.globalCompositeOperation='lighter';drawFluidStrand(context,path,tail,head,{color:profile.outer,width:15*power,offset:0,wave:2.2,blur:18,alpha:.16,quality});drawFluidStrand(context,path,tail,head,{color:profile.body,width:8*power,offset:0,wave:1.35,blur:10,alpha:.48,quality});drawFluidStrand(context,path,tail,head,{color:profile.core,width:3.6*power,offset:0,wave:.75,blur:6,alpha:.92,quality});drawFluidStrand(context,path,Math.max(tail,head-.22),head,{color:profile.tip,width:1.25,offset:0,wave:.35,blur:3,alpha:1,quality});if(quality>.65){drawFluidStrand(context,path,Math.min(head,tail+.04),head,{color:profile.outer,width:1.15,offset:-5,wave:2.8,blur:5,alpha:.46,quality});drawFluidStrand(context,path,Math.min(head,tail+.08),head,{color:profile.body,width:.9,offset:5,wave:2.1,blur:4,alpha:.52,quality})}const tip=fluidPathPoint(...path,head,0,.7),halo=context.createRadialGradient(tip.x,tip.y,0,tip.x,tip.y,11);halo.addColorStop(0,profile.tip);halo.addColorStop(.25,profile.halo);halo.addColorStop(1,'rgba(30,15,45,0)');context.fillStyle=halo;context.beginPath();context.arc(tip.x,tip.y,11,0,Math.PI*2);context.fill();if(cardTarget&&!route.first)cardTarget.classList.add('persona-card-linked');frameCount++;if(progress<1)requestAnimationFrame(frame);else resolve()}requestAnimationFrame(frame)});
  if(frameCount&&resolutionPlaybackRate===1&&duration/frameCount>25)personaFlowQualityScale=Math.max(.5,personaFlowQualityScale*.72);const finalPoint=center(target.getBoundingClientRect()),impact=document.createElement('i');impact.className=`persona-impact-ring ${profile.kind}`;impact.style.left=`${finalPoint.x}px`;impact.style.top=`${finalPoint.y}px`;document.body.append(impact);window.gameSfx?.('personaImpact');source.classList.remove('persona-charging');source.classList.add('persona-released');target.classList.add('number-receiving');document.querySelector('.score-preview')?.classList.add('persona-receiving');setTimeout(()=>{source.classList.remove('persona-released');target.classList.remove('number-receiving');cardTarget?.classList.remove('persona-card-linked');document.querySelector('.score-preview')?.classList.remove('persona-receiving');canvas.remove();callout.remove();impact.remove()},650/resolutionPlaybackRate)
}
async function tweenResolutionNumber(element,from,to,duration=480,formatter=value=>String(value)){if(!element)return;if(document.documentElement.classList.contains('reduce-motion')){element.textContent=formatter(to);return}let elapsed=0,lastFrame=performance.now();await new Promise(resolve=>{function frame(now){elapsed+=(now-lastFrame)*resolutionPlaybackRate;lastFrame=now;const progress=Math.min(1,elapsed/duration),eased=1-Math.pow(1-progress,3);element.textContent=formatter(from+(to-from)*eased);if(progress<1)requestAnimationFrame(frame);else resolve()}requestAnimationFrame(frame)})}
function scoringCardElement(event){return event.cardUid?[...document.querySelectorAll('#played-cards .played-card')].find(card=>card.dataset.uid===String(event.cardUid))||null:null}
async function animateScoringCard(event){const card=scoringCardElement(event);if(!card){pulseResolutionEvent(event);return}const chips=Number(event.chipsDelta||0),badge=document.createElement('span');badge.className=`card-chip-float ${chips?'':'zero'}`;badge.textContent=chips?`+${Number(chips.toFixed(2))} 筹码`:'0 筹码';badge.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');card.append(badge);card.classList.add('scoring-card-active');$('#message').innerHTML=`<span></span>计分牌 ${event.source} · ${badge.textContent}`;window.gameSfx?.('tick');await waitResolution(document.documentElement.classList.contains('reduce-motion')?80:290);card.classList.remove('scoring-card-active');card.classList.add('scoring-card-spent');setTimeout(()=>badge.remove(),940/resolutionPlaybackRate)}
function pulseResolutionEvent(event){
  replayClass(document.querySelector('.score-preview'),'resolution-pulse',360/resolutionPlaybackRate);
  const personaIndex=personaRuntime.getEquippedPersonas().findIndex(instance=>personaRuntime.getTemplate(instance.templateId)?.name===event.source);
  if(personaIndex>=0){
    replayClass(document.querySelectorAll('.persona')[personaIndex],'persona-hit',540/resolutionPlaybackRate);
    window.gameSfx?.('persona');
  }else window.gameSfx?.('tick');
}
async function collectPlayedCards(){
  const pile=$('#table-pile'),target=pile.getBoundingClientRect(),cards=[...document.querySelectorAll('#played-cards .played-card')];if(!cards.length)return;
  if(document.documentElement.classList.contains('reduce-motion')){usedCount+=cards.length;usedCards.push(...currentPlayed.map(card=>({...card})));currentPlayed=[];renderPlayedCards();$('#used-count').textContent=usedCount;return}
  pile.classList.add('receiving');
  await Promise.all(cards.map(async(card,index)=>{await wait(index*85);const rect=card.getBoundingClientRect(),dx=target.left+20-(rect.left+rect.width/2),dy=target.top+26-(rect.top+rect.height/2);const animation=card.animate([{transform:'translate(0,0) scale(1)',opacity:1},{transform:`translate(${dx}px,${dy}px) scale(.22) rotate(${index%2?18:-18}deg)`,opacity:.15}],{duration:420,easing:'cubic-bezier(.55,.05,.7,.25)',fill:'forwards'});await animation.finished.catch(()=>{})}));
  usedCount+=cards.length;usedCards.push(...currentPlayed.map(card=>({...card})));currentPlayed=[];pile.classList.remove('receiving');renderPlayedCards();$('#used-count').textContent=usedCount;
}
async function drawCardsAnimated(){
  const targetHandSize=battleHandSize();if(document.documentElement.classList.contains('reduce-motion')){drawTo(targetHandSize);sortHandCards();render();return}
  const pile=$('#table-pile');while(hand.length<targetHandSize&&deck.length){const card=deck.pop();hand.push(card);sortHandCards();render();const element=document.querySelector(`#cards [data-uid="${card.uid}"]`),from=pile.getBoundingClientRect(),to=element?.getBoundingClientRect();if(element&&to){const dx=from.left+20-(to.left+to.width/2),dy=from.top+25-(to.top+to.height/2);const animation=element.animate([{transform:`translate(${dx}px,${dy}px) scale(.35) rotate(-14deg)`,opacity:.15},{transform:'translate(0,0) scale(1) rotate(0)',opacity:1}],{duration:300,easing:'cubic-bezier(.2,.78,.25,1)',fill:'both'});window.gameSfx?.('select');await animation.finished.catch(()=>{})}else await wait(180);await wait(65)}
}
async function dealOpeningHandAnimated(){
  const cards=[...document.querySelectorAll('#cards .playing-card')],pile=$('#table-pile');$('#cards').classList.remove('opening-deal-pending');if(!cards.length)return;
  if(document.documentElement.classList.contains('reduce-motion')){cards.forEach(card=>card.style.opacity='');return}
  const from=pile.getBoundingClientRect();cards.forEach(card=>card.style.opacity='0');$('#cards').classList.add('opening-deal-active');pile.classList.add('dealing');
  await Promise.all(cards.map(async(card,index)=>{await wait(index*115);const to=card.getBoundingClientRect(),endTransform=getComputedStyle(card).transform==='none'?'translate(0,0)':getComputedStyle(card).transform,dx=from.left+from.width/2-(to.left+to.width/2),dy=from.top+from.height/2-(to.top+to.height/2);card.style.opacity='';const animation=card.animate([{transform:`translate(${dx}px,${dy}px) scale(.18) rotate(${-22+index*4}deg)`,opacity:0,filter:'brightness(1.8)'},{offset:.72,opacity:1,filter:'brightness(1.15)'},{transform:endTransform,opacity:1,filter:'brightness(1)'}],{duration:520,easing:'cubic-bezier(.18,.76,.25,1)',fill:'both'});window.gameSfx?.('select');await animation.finished.catch(()=>{});animation.cancel()}));
  $('#cards').classList.remove('opening-deal-active');pile.classList.remove('dealing');
}
function resolveScore(cards,consume=false){
  return battleScoreRuntime.resolve({cards,consume,handTypes:currentHandTypes(),growthState:currentShopGrowthState(),stageLimitInstance:activeStageLimit(),stageLimitView:activeStageLimitView(),context:{runTemplateId:currentRunTemplate().id,nodeId:currentStageNode()?.id,encounterId:currentEncounterDefinition()?.id,handIndex:battlePlayed+1,currentHandCardCount:hand.length,previousHandType:lastHandType,previousHandTypeId:lastHandTypeId,remainingHands:hands,remainingDiscards:discards,disabledSlotIndexes:[]}});
}
async function animateScoreResolution(result){
  const reduced=document.documentElement.classList.contains('reduce-motion'),personaEvents=result.events.filter(event=>event.phase==='人格牌'&&!event.detail.includes('禁用')),chainPanel=$('#persona-chain');let chips=0,mult=0,xmult=1,personaIndex=0;resolutionAnimating=true;beginResolutionPlayback();renderScoreBreakdown(result.breakdown);
  try{for(const event of result.events){
      if(event.phase==='汇总'||event.phase==='人格调试')continue;
      const previous={chips,mult,xmult,score:Math.max(1,Math.round(chips*mult*xmult))};if(event.chipsDelta)chips+=event.chipsDelta;if(event.multDelta)mult+=event.multDelta;if(event.xmultFactor)xmult*=event.xmultFactor;const nextScore=Math.max(1,Math.round(chips*mult*xmult));
      $('#preview-type').textContent=result.type;$('#message').innerHTML=`<span></span>${event.phase} · ${event.source}：${event.detail}`;
      if(event.phase==='人格牌'){const chain={index:personaIndex++,total:personaEvents.length};if(chain.total>1){chainPanel.textContent=`人格连锁 ${chain.index+1} / ${chain.total}`;chainPanel.classList.remove('hidden')}await animatePersonaFluid(event,chain)}else if(event.phase==='计分牌')await animateScoringCard(event);else pulseResolutionEvent(event);
      const numberDuration=event.phase==='人格牌'?560:240;
      if(event.chipsDelta)await tweenResolutionNumber($('#preview-chips'),previous.chips,chips,numberDuration,value=>String(Math.round(value)));else $('#preview-chips').textContent=chips;
      if(event.multDelta||event.xmultFactor)await tweenResolutionNumber($('#preview-mult'),previous.mult*xmult,mult*xmult,numberDuration,value=>`×${Number(value.toFixed(2))}`);else $('#preview-mult').textContent=`×${mult}${xmult!==1?` ×${Number(xmult.toFixed(2))}`:''}`;
      await tweenResolutionNumber($('#preview-score'),previous.score,nextScore,event.phase==='人格牌'?520:220,value=>String(Math.round(value)));
      if(!reduced)await waitResolution(event.phase==='人格牌'?170:event.phase==='计分牌'?100:180);
    }
    $('#preview-chips').textContent=uiFormatters.formatBattleNumber(result.chips);$('#preview-mult').textContent=`×${uiFormatters.formatBattleNumber(result.mult)}${result.xmult!==1?` ×${uiFormatters.formatBattleNumber(result.xmult)}`:''}`;$('#preview-score').textContent=Math.round(result.total);replayClass($('#preview-score'),'final-score-hit',520/resolutionPlaybackRate);$('#message').innerHTML=`<span></span>${result.type} · 本次获得 ${Math.round(result.total)} 分`;
    replayClass(document.querySelector('.battle-stage'),'battle-impact',320/resolutionPlaybackRate);showBattleFloat(`+${result.total}`);window.gameSfx?.('score');if(!reduced)await waitResolution(620)
  }finally{endResolutionPlayback();chainPanel.classList.add('hidden');chainPanel.textContent=''}
}
async function play(){
  if(inputLocked)return;inputLocked=true;window.gameSfx?.('play');
  const cards=[...selected].map(i=>hand[i]),result=resolveScore(cards,true),feedback=globalThis.PersonaFeedback.feedbackFromLogs(result.personaLogs,{...personaFeedbackContext(),history:result.previewHistory,handTypeId:result.typeId,handTypeName:result.type,kind:'commit'});recordPlay(cards,result);currentPlayed=cards.map(card=>({...card}));animatePlayed=true;if(new Set(cards.map(c=>c.si)).size>=2)suitPlays++;
  hands--;played++;battlePlayed++;lastHandType=result.type;lastHandTypeId=result.typeId;updateStageLimit(stageLimitRuntime?.afterPlay(activeStageLimit()));if(result.pair)pairs++;if(result.goldDelta){coins+=result.goldDelta;earnedThisBattle+=result.goldDelta;cardGoldThisBattle+=result.cardGoldDelta;personaGoldThisBattle+=result.personaGoldDelta;behavior.earnedCoins+=result.goldDelta;if(result.cardGoldDelta)targetRawEvent('CARD_GOLD_EARNED',{amount:result.cardGoldDelta,cardUids:result.scoringCards.filter(card=>globalThis.ShopRuntime.cardUpgradeAttributes(card).bonusCoins).map(card=>card.uid)});if(result.personaGoldDelta)targetRawEvent('PERSONA_GOLD_EARNED',{amount:result.personaGoldDelta})}removeSelected();render();showPersonaTriggerFeedback(feedback);await animateScoreResolution(result);score+=result.total;render();await collectPlayedCards();if(score>=battleTarget()){inputLocked=false;render();finish(true);return}if(!hands){inputLocked=false;render();finish(false);return}await drawCardsAnimated();inputLocked=false;render();window.commitRunSave?.('battle','play_resolved')
}
async function discard(){
  if(inputLocked)return;inputLocked=true;window.gameSfx?.('discard');const thrown=[...selected].map(i=>hand[i]),n=thrown.length;recordDiscard(n);discardedCards.push(...thrown.map(card=>({...card})));hand=hand.filter((_,i)=>!selected.has(i));selected.clear();usedCount+=n;
  const runtimeResult=personaRuntime.processDiscard(n,{commit:true,context:{runTemplateId:currentRunTemplate().id,nodeId:currentStageNode()?.id,encounterId:currentEncounterDefinition()?.id,remainingHands:hands,remainingDiscards:discards,discardedCards:thrown,disabledSlotIndexes:[]}}),feedback=globalThis.PersonaFeedback.discardFeedback(runtimeResult.logs,personaFeedbackContext());
  discards--;updateStageLimit(stageLimitRuntime?.afterDiscard(activeStageLimit()));$('#message').innerHTML=`<span></span>已弃掉 ${n} 张牌，正在重新补牌。`;render();showPersonaTriggerFeedback(feedback);await drawCardsAnimated();inputLocked=false;render();window.commitRunSave?.('battle','discard_resolved')
}
function battleCoinBreakdown(){const reward=currentRunTemplate()?.actionRules?.battleReward||{perRemainingHand:1,perRemainingDiscard:0},baseVictoryCoins=currentStageNode()?.victoryCoins||0,victoryCoins=stageLimitRuntime?.adjustVictoryCoins(activeStageLimit(),baseVictoryCoins)??baseVictoryCoins,handRate=reward.perRemainingHand||0,discardRate=reward.perRemainingDiscard||0,handCoins=handRate*hands,discardCoins=discardRate*discards;return{baseVictoryCoins,victoryCoins,remainingHands:hands,handRate,handCoins,remainingDiscards:discards,discardRate,discardCoins,rewardTotal:victoryCoins+handCoins+discardCoins}}
function battleCoinReward(){return battleCoinBreakdown().rewardTotal}
function finish(win){
  inputLocked=true;const applyResolution=()=>{recordBattleEnd(win);if(win){const reward=battleCoinReward();earnedThisBattle+=reward;coins+=reward;behavior.earnedCoins+=reward}};
  const once=globalThis.runController?.getState()?globalThis.runController.runOnce('battle_resolution',applyResolution):{executed:true,value:applyResolution()};if(!once.executed)return;
  $('#play-btn').disabled=true;$('#discard-btn').disabled=true;$('#message').innerHTML=`<span></span>${win?'你穿过了这一层镜厅。':'牌局结束，但你的选择已留下倒影。'}`;setTimeout(()=>openSettlement(win),450)
}

let settlementWasWin=false,settlementNodeId=null;
function animateSettlementRewards(total){const dialog=$('#settlement-dialog'),output=$('#result-coins');dialog.classList.remove('reward-sequence');void dialog.offsetWidth;dialog.classList.add('reward-sequence');if(document.documentElement.classList.contains('reduce-motion')||typeof globalThis.requestAnimationFrame!=='function'){output.textContent=total;return}output.textContent='0';setTimeout(()=>{const started=Date.now(),duration=760;function tick(){const progress=Math.min(1,(Date.now()-started)/duration),eased=1-Math.pow(1-progress,3);output.textContent=Math.round(total*eased);if(progress<1)globalThis.requestAnimationFrame(tick)}globalThis.requestAnimationFrame(tick)},520)}
function openSettlement(win,persist=true){
  settlementWasWin=win;settlementNodeId=currentStageNode()?.id||settlementNodeId;
  const dialog=$('#settlement-dialog'),target=battleTarget(),transition=globalThis.runController?.resolveTransition(runNodeById.get(settlementNodeId),{type:win?'BATTLE_WIN':'BATTLE_LOSS'}),targetNode=runNodeById.get(transition?.to),personaMilestone=win&&isTargetRun()&&targetNode?.type==='PERSONA_GROWTH',coinBreakdown=battleCoinBreakdown(),cardCoins=cardGoldThisBattle,personaCoins=personaGoldThisBattle;
  dialog.dataset.result=win?'success':'failure';$('#result-emblem').textContent=win?'♛':'♢';$('#result-title').textContent=win?'战斗胜利':'战斗失败';$('#result-subtitle').textContent=win?(personaMilestone?'完成阶段挑战，获得一张人格牌。':isTargetRun()?'目标节点已完成，获得本战金币。':'镜厅暂时承认了你的选择。'):'你未能在出牌耗尽前达到目标。';$('#result-score').textContent=score;$('#result-target').textContent=target;$('#result-victory-formula').textContent=`固定奖励 ${coinBreakdown.victoryCoins} 金币`;$('#result-victory-coins').textContent=coinBreakdown.victoryCoins;$('#result-hands').textContent=coinBreakdown.remainingHands;$('#result-hand-rate').textContent=coinBreakdown.handRate;$('#result-hand-coins').textContent=coinBreakdown.handCoins;$('#result-discards').textContent=coinBreakdown.remainingDiscards;$('#result-discard-rate').textContent=coinBreakdown.discardRate;$('#result-discard-coins').textContent=coinBreakdown.discardCoins;$('#result-card-coins').textContent=cardCoins;$('#result-card-coins-reward').classList.toggle('hidden',!cardCoins);$('#result-persona-coins').textContent=personaCoins;$('#result-persona-coins-reward').classList.toggle('hidden',!personaCoins);$('#result-coins').textContent=earnedThisBattle;$('#result-current-coins').textContent=coins;$('#result-gap').textContent=Math.max(0,target-score);$('#result-rewards').classList.toggle('hidden',!win);$('#result-persona-reward').classList.toggle('hidden',!personaMilestone);$('#result-primary').textContent=personaMilestone?'确定':targetNode?.type==='ROUTE'?'领取奖励':targetNode?.type==='PERSONA_GROWTH'?'进入成长节点':targetNode?.type==='END'?'结束测试':targetNode?.type==='REPORT'?'查看目标长局报告':'进入下一战';dialog.showModal();if(win)animateSettlementRewards(earnedThisBattle);if(persist)window.commitRunSave?.('settlement','battle_finished');window.gameMusicStinger?.(win?'victory':'failure');window.gameSfx?.(win?'win':'discard')
}
function continueAfterSettlement(){const dialog=$('#settlement-dialog');if(dialog.open)dialog.close();globalThis.runController.completeNode({type:settlementWasWin?'BATTLE_WIN':'BATTLE_LOSS',nodeId:settlementNodeId,score,targetScore:battleTarget(),remainingHands:hands,remainingDiscards:discards})}
function retryAfterSettlement(){if($('#settlement-dialog').open)$('#settlement-dialog').close();reset();globalThis.runController.startRun(balance.meta.activeRunTemplateId)}
function menuAfterSettlement(){if($('#settlement-dialog').open)$('#settlement-dialog').close();globalThis.runController.finishRun({type:'RUN_ABANDONED',reason:'settlement_menu'})}

function openRouteMap(persist=true){
  if(!currentRouteNodes.length){nextTargetModifier=1;generateRouteNodes()}
  $('#route-next-index').textContent=String(battleIndex+2).padStart(2,'0');
  $('#route-boss-index').textContent=String(battleIndex+2).padStart(2,'0');
  $('#route-summary').textContent=`已突破镜厅节点 ${battleIndex+1}，请选择一项下一战准备。`;
