  globalThis.runController?.setNodeRuntime({routeChoices:currentRouteNodes,randomEvent:currentRandomEvent});renderRouteNodes();window.showRouteMap?.();if(persist)window.commitRunSave?.('route','route_opened');window.gameSfx?.('win');
}
const routeNodeDefs={
  shop:{icon:'¤',kicker:'幕间整备',name:'幕间商店',copy:'使用本局获得的金币整理牌库、强化卡牌，或为下一战增加弃牌次数。',effect:'进入商店'},
  rest:{icon:'☾',kicker:'静谧之室',name:'静思室',copy:'跳过消费，在下一场战斗额外获得 1 次弃牌机会。',effect:'获得：下场弃牌 +1'},
  engrave:{icon:'◇',kicker:'镜面刻印',name:'镜之刻印',copy:'随机选择牌库中的一张牌，使其在后续战斗中永久获得 +3 计分筹码。',effect:'获得：随机卡牌 +3'},
  event:{icon:'◉',kicker:'未知倒影',name:'未知倒影',copy:'接近一面不属于首领的镜子，从两种代价与收益中作出选择。',effect:'揭示随机事件'}
};
function generateRouteNodes(){
  currentRouteNodes=['shop',...shuffle(['rest','engrave','event']).slice(0,2)];
  if(currentRouteNodes.includes('event')){const template=pick(routeEventPool);currentRandomEvent={...template,options:template.options.map(option=>({...option}))}}
}
function renderRouteNodes(){
  const defaults=['shop','rest','engrave'];
  [...document.querySelectorAll('[data-route-node]')].forEach((button,index)=>{const type=currentRouteNodes[index]||defaults[index],node=routeNodeDefs[type];button.dataset.routeNode=type;button.className=`route-node ${type}-node`;button.disabled=false;button.innerHTML=`<span class="route-node-icon">${node.icon}</span><h2>${node.name}</h2><p>${node.copy}</p><b>${node.effect}</b>`;button.onclick=()=>chooseRouteNode(type)})
}
function strengthenRandomRunCard(amount=3){
  if(!runDeck.length)return null;
  const card=runDeck[Math.floor(Math.random()*runDeck.length)];card.bonus=(card.bonus||0)+amount;return card;
}
function strengthenRandomRunCards(count=2,amount=3){const available=[...runDeck],result=[];while(result.length<count&&available.length){const index=Math.floor(Math.random()*available.length),card=available.splice(index,1)[0];card.bonus=(card.bonus||0)+amount;result.push(card)}return result}
function chooseRouteNode(type){
  if(type==='shop'){window.showBattleFromRoute?.();openShop();return}
  if(type==='event'){openRandomEvent();return}
  if(type==='rest'){$('#route-summary').textContent='静思完成：下一场弃牌次数 +1。';extraDiscard+=1;window.gameSfx?.('persona')}
  if(type==='engrave'){const card=strengthenRandomRunCard();$('#route-summary').textContent=card?`刻印完成：${card.r}${card.s} 在后续战斗中获得 +3 计分筹码。`:'牌库中没有可刻印的卡牌。';window.gameSfx?.('forge')}
  document.querySelectorAll('[data-route-node]').forEach(button=>button.disabled=true);
  setTimeout(()=>completeRouteNode({routeType:type}),650);
}
function openRandomEvent(persist=true){
  if(!currentRandomEvent){const template=pick(routeEventPool);currentRandomEvent={...template,options:template.options.map(option=>({...option}))}}
  const event=currentRandomEvent;$('#event-emblem').textContent=event.icon;$('#event-title').textContent=event.name;$('#event-copy').textContent=event.copy;$('#event-note').textContent=event.outcome||'选择之后无法反悔。';
  $('#event-options').innerHTML=event.options.map(option=>`<button class="event-option" data-event-option="${option.id}" ${event.resolved||option.minCoins>coins?'disabled':''}><b>${option.title}</b><span>${option.desc}</span><em>${option.effect}${option.minCoins>coins?' · 金币不足':''}</em></button>`).join('');
  globalThis.runController?.setNodeRuntime({routeChoices:currentRouteNodes,randomEvent:currentRandomEvent});document.querySelectorAll('[data-event-option]').forEach(button=>button.onclick=()=>resolveRandomEvent(button.dataset.eventOption));const dialog=$('#event-dialog');if(!dialog.open)dialog.showModal();if(persist)window.commitRunSave?.('event','random_event_opened');if(event.resolved)setTimeout(()=>completeRouteNode({routeType:'event',eventId:event.id,outcome:event.outcome}),850);window.gameSfx?.('boss')
}
function resolveRandomEvent(optionId){
  const event=currentRandomEvent,option=event?.options.find(item=>item.id===optionId);if(!event||!option||event.resolved||option.minCoins>coins)return false;let outcome='倒影接受了你的选择。';
  if(event.id==='watching_eye'&&optionId==='offer'){coins-=3;behavior.spentCoins+=3;const card=strengthenRandomRunCard(5);outcome=`你献上 3 金币，${card.r}${card.s} 获得 +5 计分筹码。`}
  else if(event.id==='watching_eye')outcome='你移开视线，没有付出，也没有获得。';
  else if(event.id==='broken_clock'&&optionId==='wind'){extraHand+=1;nextTargetModifier=1.1;outcome='时钟再次走动：下一场出牌 +1，目标分数 +10%。'}
  else if(event.id==='broken_clock'){coins+=4;behavior.earnedCoins+=4;outcome='你拆下齿轮，获得 4 金币。'}
  else if(event.id==='nameless_card'&&optionId==='inscribe'){const cards=strengthenRandomRunCards(2,3);outcome=`你写下名字：${cards.map(card=>`${card.r}${card.s}`).join('、')} 各获得 +3 计分筹码。`}
  else{coins+=3;behavior.earnedCoins+=3;outcome='你保持空白，获得 3 金币。'}
  event.resolved=true;event.outcome=outcome;globalThis.runController?.setNodeRuntime({randomEvent:currentRandomEvent});$('#event-note').textContent=outcome;document.querySelectorAll('[data-event-option]').forEach(button=>button.disabled=true);window.commitRunSave?.('event','random_event_resolved');window.gameSfx?.('forge');setTimeout(()=>completeRouteNode({routeType:'event',eventId:event.id,optionId,outcome}),850);return true
}

function shopPersonaEntries(){let state=personaRuntime.getState(),pool=state?.runPersonaPool||[];for(const id of pool){const instance=state.personaInstancesById[id],template=instance&&personaRuntime.getTemplate(instance.templateId),inherited=inheritedPersonaAffixTemplate(template);if(template&&inherited!==template)personaRuntime.registerTemplate(inherited)}state=personaRuntime.getState();pool=state?.runPersonaPool||[];if(pool.length)return pool.map(id=>{const instance=state.personaInstancesById[id],template=instance&&personaRuntime.getTemplate(instance.templateId);return instance&&template?{instance,template}:null}).filter(Boolean);return equippedPersonaIds.map(id=>{const template=runManifest.basePersonas.templates.find(item=>item.id===id);return template?{instance:null,template}:null}).filter(Boolean)}
function shopPersonaPresentation(template,instance){return personaPresentation.shopCardView(template,instance,{feedbackCardView:(target,state)=>globalThis.PersonaFeedback.cardView(target,state)})}
function shopPersonaEffectText(presentation){return personaPresentation.shopEffectText(presentation)}
function shopPersonaPortraitStyle(template){return template.portrait?`style="--portrait:url('${escapePersonaUiText(template.portrait)}')"`:''}
function personaForgeSlotMarkup(template,instance){const slots=personaAffixSlots(template,instance),profileId=currentShopProfileId();return slots.map(slot=>{const affix=slot.affixId&&personaRuntime.getSubAffix(slot.affixId),locked=!slot.unlocked,availability=locked&&typeof personaRuntime.getSubAffixUnlockAvailability==='function'?personaRuntime.getSubAffixUnlockAvailability(instance.instanceId,slot.slotIndex,{profileId}):locked?{allowed:slot.slotIndex===0||slots[slot.slotIndex-1]?.unlocked}:null,canUnlock=!!availability?.allowed,label=`副属性${slot.slotIndex+1}`,content=locked?'未解锁':affix?.effectText||'已解锁',action=locked?`<button type="button" ${canUnlock?'':'disabled'} data-unlock-persona-affix="${escapePersonaUiText(instance.instanceId)}" data-affix-slot="${slot.slotIndex}">解锁·${slot.unlockCost}金币</button>`:'<em>已解锁</em>';return`<div class="persona-forge-slot ${locked?'locked':'unlocked'}"><b>${label}</b><span>${escapePersonaUiText(content)}</span>${action}</div>`}).join('')}
function currentShopSession(){return globalThis.runController?.getCurrentRuntime()?.data?.shopSession||null}
function validShopSession(session,profileId){const expectedSlots=shopConfig.refreshProfiles.find(profile=>profile.id===profileId)?.offerSlotCount;return session?.version===2&&session.profileId===profileId&&Number.isInteger(session.refreshIndex)&&session.refreshIndex>=0&&Array.isArray(session.purchasedItemIds)&&Array.isArray(session.offers)&&session.offers.length===expectedSlots&&session.offers.every(offer=>typeof offer.offerId==='string'&&shopItemById.has(offer.itemId)&&Number.isInteger(offer.purchaseCount)&&offer.purchaseCount>=0)}
function currentShopProfileId(){const node=currentStageNode();return node?.shopProfileId||(shopConfig.refreshProfiles[Math.min(Math.max(0,battleIndex),shopConfig.refreshProfiles.length-1)]?.id||'AI1')}
function ensureShopSession(){
  const profileId=currentShopProfileId(),runtimeData=globalThis.runController.getCurrentRuntime()?.data||{};
  if(validShopSession(runtimeData.shopSession,profileId))return runtimeData.shopSession;
  const generated=globalThis.ShopRuntime.generateOffers({config:shopConfig,profileId,random:Math.random,refreshIndex:0}),session={...generated,selectedItemId:generated.offers[0]?.itemId||null,migratedFromLegacyStaticShop:!!runtimeData.shopSession};
  globalThis.runController.setNodeRuntime({shopSession:session});
  return session;
}
function shopOffer(session,itemId){return session?.offers?.find(offer=>offer.itemId===itemId)||null}
function currentShopGrowthState(){return globalThis.runController.getShopGrowthState?.()||globalThis.ShopRuntime.normalizeGrowthState()}
function shopUpgradeTargetLevel(item,targetId){if(!item?.priceGrowth||!targetId)return 0;if(item.effect.type==='UPGRADE_PERSONA_MAIN')return Number(personaRuntime.getState().personaInstancesById[targetId]?.shopMainStatUpgrade?.level||0);const growth=currentShopGrowthState();if(item.effect.type==='UPGRADE_SUIT')return Number(growth.suitLevelsBySuit?.[targetId]||0);if(item.effect.type==='UPGRADE_HAND_TYPE')return Number(growth.handTypeLevelsById?.[targetId]||0);return 0}
function shopItemPrice(item,targetId=null){return globalThis.ShopRuntime.targetUpgradePrice(item,shopUpgradeTargetLevel(item,targetId))}
function updateShopSelection(itemId){const session=ensureShopSession();if(!shopOffer(session,itemId))return;selectedShopItemId=itemId;globalThis.runController.setNodeRuntime({shopSession:{...session,selectedItemId:itemId}})}
function shopItemView(item){
  const serviceIcon={BONUS_CHIPS:'↑',BONUS_COINS:'◉',BONUS_MULT:'×',BONUS_XMULT_RATE:'✦'}[item.effect?.targetStat]||({REMOVE_CARD:'✕',UPGRADE_PERSONA_MAIN:'◇',UPGRADE_SUIT:'♠',UPGRADE_HAND_TYPE:'♣'}[item.effect?.type]||'◆'),icons={CARD:item.effect?.card?.suitSymbol||'▰',PERSONA:'◇',SERVICE:serviceIcon},template=item.itemType==='PERSONA'?personaRuntime.getTemplate(item.effect.personaTemplateId):null,name=template&&template.name!==item.name?`${item.name} · ${template.name}`:item.name,effect=globalThis.ShopRuntime.describeEffect(item);
  return{name,icon:icons[item.itemType]||'◆',effect}
}
function shopPlayingCard(item){const config=item.itemType==='CARD'&&item.effect?.card;if(!config)return null;const suit=config.suitSymbol,rank=String(config.rank);return{s:suit,r:rank,c:['♥','♦'].includes(suit)?'red':'black'}}
function shopItemIconMarkup(item,view){const card=shopPlayingCard(item),art=card&&cardArtPath(card);return art?`<span class="shop-icon has-playing-card">${cardArtImage(card,'shop-card-thumbnail')}</span>`:`<span class="shop-icon">${escapePersonaUiText(view.icon)}</span>`}
function renderShopDetail(item,offer){
  const price=shopItemPrice(item),effectiveItem={...item,price},purchased=(ensureShopSession().purchasedItemIds||[]).includes(item.id),availability=globalThis.ShopRuntime.purchaseAvailability({item:effectiveItem,coins,purchaseCount:purchased?item.purchaseLimit:offer.purchaseCount}),view=shopItemView(item),card=shopPlayingCard(item),cardArt=card&&cardArtPath(card);
  const icon=$('#shop-detail-icon');icon.classList.remove('has-art');icon.classList.toggle('has-playing-card',Boolean(cardArt));icon.style.removeProperty('--portrait');icon.innerHTML=cardArt?cardArtImage(card,'shop-card-preview'):escapePersonaUiText(view.icon);$('#shop-detail-name').textContent=view.name;$('#shop-detail-effect').textContent=item.priceGrowth?`${view.effect} 当前价格从 ${item.price} 金币起，目标每强化1级增加 ${item.priceGrowth.increment} 金币。`:view.effect;$('#shop-buy').disabled=!availability.allowed;$('#shop-buy').textContent=availability.reason==='PURCHASE_LIMIT_REACHED'?'已购买':availability.reason==='INSUFFICIENT_COINS'?'金币不足':item.priceGrowth?`选择目标（${item.price} 金币起）`:`购买商品（${price} 金币）`;
}
function selectShopItem(itemId){
  const session=ensureShopSession(),offer=shopOffer(session,itemId),item=shopItemById.get(itemId);if(!offer||!item)return;
  updateShopSelection(itemId);document.querySelectorAll('[data-shop-item]').forEach(button=>{const active=button.dataset.shopItem===itemId;button.classList.toggle('selected',active);button.setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(active))});renderShopDetail(item,offer);window.gameSfx?.('select');
}
function renderShopItems(session){
  const list=$('#shop-item-list'),scrollTop=list.scrollTop;
  list.innerHTML=session.offers.map(offer=>{const item=shopItemById.get(offer.itemId),view=shopItemView(item),sold=(session.purchasedItemIds||[]).includes(item.id)||offer.purchaseCount>=item.purchaseLimit,priceText=item.priceGrowth?`${item.price}+`:String(item.price);return`<button type="button" class="shop-item ${item.id===selectedShopItemId?'selected':''} ${sold?'sold':''}" data-shop-item="${escapePersonaUiText(item.id)}" aria-label="${escapePersonaUiText(view.name)}，${priceText} 金币${sold?'，已购买':''}" aria-pressed="${item.id===selectedShopItemId}">${shopItemIconMarkup(item,view)}<span class="shop-item-summary"><h4>${escapePersonaUiText(view.name)}</h4><small>◉ ${priceText}</small></span></button>`}).join('')||'<p class="shop-empty-state">本次商店没有可用商品。</p>';
  document.querySelectorAll('[data-shop-item]').forEach(button=>button.onclick=()=>selectShopItem(button.dataset.shopItem));list.scrollTop=Math.min(scrollTop,Math.max(0,list.scrollHeight-list.clientHeight));
}
function renderShopForgeDetail(entry){const {instance,template}=entry,presentation=shopPersonaPresentation(template,instance),icon=$('#shop-detail-icon');icon.textContent=template.portrait?'':'◇';icon.classList.remove('has-playing-card');icon.classList.toggle('has-art',Boolean(template.portrait));if(template.portrait)icon.style.setProperty('--portrait',`url('${template.portrait}')`);else icon.style.removeProperty('--portrait');$('#shop-detail-name').textContent=template.displayId||template.name;$('#shop-detail-effect').textContent=shopPersonaEffectText(presentation);$('#shop-result').innerHTML=presentation.hasAffixes&&instance?personaForgeSlotMarkup(template,instance):'<p class="shop-persona-static-result">该人格没有可铸造的副属性。</p>';document.querySelectorAll('[data-unlock-persona-affix]').forEach(button=>button.onclick=()=>unlockPersonaAffix(button.dataset.unlockPersonaAffix,Number(button.dataset.affixSlot)))}
function unlockPersonaAffix(instanceId,slotIndex){const instance=personaRuntime.getState().personaInstancesById[instanceId],template=instance&&personaRuntime.getTemplate(instance.templateId),slot=template&&personaAffixSlots(template,instance)[slotIndex],cost=slot?.unlockCost;if(!slot||slot.unlocked)return false;if(!Number.isFinite(cost)||coins<cost){$('#shop-note').textContent='解锁失败：金币不足。';window.gameSfx?.('discard');return false}try{const effectId=`PERSONA_ATTRIBUTE_UNLOCK:${instanceId}:${slotIndex}`,once=globalThis.runController.runOnce(effectId,()=>{const unlocked=personaRuntime.unlockSubAffix(instanceId,slotIndex,{profileId:currentShopProfileId()});coins-=cost;behavior.spentCoins+=cost;targetRawEvent('PERSONA_ATTRIBUTE_UNLOCKED',{instanceId,templateId:instance.templateId,attributeId:unlocked.affixId,attributePosition:unlocked.attributePosition||slotIndex+2,cost,profileId:currentShopProfileId()});return unlocked});if(!once.executed)return false;const attribute=personaRuntime.getSubAffix(once.value.affixId),position=once.value.attributePosition||slotIndex+2;$('#shop-note').textContent=`第 ${position} 属性已解锁：${attribute.effectText}（消耗 ${cost} 金币）。`;$('#shop-coins').textContent=coins;renderShopForge();window.commitRunSave?.('shop','persona_attribute_unlocked');window.gameSfx?.('forge');return true}catch(error){$('#shop-note').textContent=`解锁失败：${error.message}`;window.gameSfx?.('discard');return false}}
function selectShopPersona(id,entries){selectedShopPersonaId=id;document.querySelectorAll('[data-shop-persona]').forEach(button=>{const active=button.dataset.shopPersona===id;button.classList.toggle('selected',active);button.setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(active))});const entry=entries.find(({instance,template})=>(instance?.instanceId||template.id)===id);if(entry)renderShopForgeDetail(entry);window.gameSfx?.('select')}
function renderShopForge(){const entries=shopPersonaEntries(),list=$('#shop-forge-list'),scrollTop=list.scrollTop;if(!entries.length){list.innerHTML='<p class="shop-empty-state">当前没有可展示的人格牌。</p>';$('#shop-detail-icon').textContent='◇';$('#shop-detail-icon').classList.remove('has-art','has-playing-card');$('#shop-detail-icon').style.removeProperty('--portrait');$('#shop-detail-name').textContent='暂无人格牌';$('#shop-detail-effect').textContent='当前人格池为空。';$('#shop-result').textContent='当前没有可铸造的人格牌。';return}if(!entries.some(({instance,template})=>(instance?.instanceId||template.id)===selectedShopPersonaId))selectedShopPersonaId=entries[0].instance?.instanceId||entries[0].template.id;list.innerHTML=entries.map(({instance,template})=>{const slots=personaAffixSlots(template,instance),presentation=shopPersonaPresentation(template,instance),id=instance?.instanceId||template.id,progress=presentation.hasAffixes?`${slots.filter(slot=>slot.unlocked).length}/${slots.length}`:'—';return`<button type="button" class="shop-forge-item ${id===selectedShopPersonaId?'selected':''}" data-shop-persona="${escapePersonaUiText(id)}" aria-pressed="${id===selectedShopPersonaId}"><span class="shop-forge-icon ${template.portrait?'has-art':''}" ${shopPersonaPortraitStyle(template)}>◇</span><div><h4>${escapePersonaUiText(template.displayId||template.name)}</h4><p>${escapePersonaUiText(shopPersonaEffectText(presentation))}</p></div><em>${progress}</em></button>`}).join('');document.querySelectorAll('[data-shop-persona]').forEach(button=>button.onclick=()=>selectShopPersona(button.dataset.shopPersona,entries));list.scrollTop=Math.min(scrollTop,Math.max(0,list.scrollHeight-list.clientHeight));renderShopForgeDetail(entries.find(({instance,template})=>(instance?.instanceId||template.id)===selectedShopPersonaId))}
function renderShop(){const session=ensureShopSession(),personaEntries=shopPersonaEntries(),refreshCost=globalThis.ShopRuntime.refreshCost(session.refreshIndex);if(!shopOffer(session,selectedShopItemId))selectedShopItemId=shopOffer(session,session.selectedItemId)?session.selectedItemId:session.offers[0]?.itemId||null;if(selectedShopItemId!==session.selectedItemId)updateShopSelection(selectedShopItemId);$('#shop-coins').textContent=coins;$('#shop-deck-count').textContent=runDeck.length;$('#shop-persona-count').textContent=personaEntries.length;$('#shop-refresh').textContent=refreshCost?`刷新商店（${refreshCost} 金币）`:'免费刷新';$('#shop-refresh').disabled=coins<refreshCost;renderShopItems(session);const offer=shopOffer(session,selectedShopItemId),item=shopItemById.get(selectedShopItemId);if(item&&offer)renderShopDetail(item,offer)}
function refreshShop(){const session=ensureShopSession(),cost=globalThis.ShopRuntime.refreshCost(session.refreshIndex);if(coins<cost){$('#shop-note').textContent='刷新失败：金币不足。';renderShop();window.gameSfx?.('discard');return false}const nextIndex=session.refreshIndex+1,purchasedItemIds=[...(session.purchasedItemIds||[])],eligibleItemIds=shopConfig.items.map(item=>item.id).filter(id=>!purchasedItemIds.includes(id)),effectId=`SHOP_REFRESH:${currentStageNode()?.id}:${nextIndex}`,once=globalThis.runController.runOnce(effectId,()=>{const generated=globalThis.ShopRuntime.generateOffers({config:shopConfig,profileId:session.profileId,random:Math.random,eligibleItemIds,refreshIndex:nextIndex}),nextSession={...generated,purchasedItemIds,selectedItemId:generated.offers[0]?.itemId||null};coins-=cost;behavior.spentCoins+=cost;behavior.refreshes++;globalThis.runController.setNodeRuntime({shopSession:nextSession});targetRawEvent('SHOP_REFRESH',{profileId:session.profileId,refreshIndex:nextIndex,cost});return nextSession});if(!once.executed)return false;selectedShopItemId=once.value.selectedItemId;$('#shop-note').textContent=cost?`已消耗 ${cost} 金币刷新商品。`:'已使用本轮免费刷新。';renderShop();window.commitRunSave?.('shop','shop_refreshed');window.gameSfx?.('select');return true}
function setShopTab(tab){shopTab=tab==='forge'?'forge':'goods';const forge=shopTab==='forge',dialog=$('#shop-dialog'),buy=$('#shop-buy'),refresh=$('#shop-refresh'),goodsPanel=$('#shop-goods-panel'),forgePanel=$('#shop-forge-panel');dialog.classList.toggle('forge-mode',forge);goodsPanel.hidden=forge;forgePanel.hidden=!forge;goodsPanel.classList.toggle('hidden',forge);forgePanel.classList.toggle('hidden',!forge);buy.hidden=forge;buy.classList.toggle('hidden',forge);refresh.hidden=forge;refresh.classList.toggle('hidden',forge);$('.shop-detail-panel').classList.remove('hidden');document.querySelectorAll('[data-shop-tab]').forEach(button=>{const active=button.dataset.shopTab===shopTab;button.classList.toggle('active',active);button.setAttribute('aria-selected',String(active))});if(forge)renderShopForge();else renderShop()}
function openShop(persist=true,restoring=false){const session=ensureShopSession();if(!restoring)behavior.shopVisits++;selectedShopItemId=shopOffer(session,session.selectedItemId)?session.selectedItemId:session.offers[0]?.itemId||null;deckShopItemId=null;shopUpgradeItemId=null;shopUpgradeTargetId=null;$('#shop-note').textContent=restoring?'已恢复本次商店库存和刷新次数，商品不会重新抽取。':`${session.profileId} 商店已刷新；首次手动刷新免费，每件商品本轮最多购买 1 次。`;setShopTab(shopTab);if(!$('#shop-dialog').open)$('#shop-dialog').showModal();if(persist)window.commitRunSave?.('shop','shop_opened')}
function recordShopPurchase(item,offer,outcome,price=item.price,targetId=null){behavior.spentCoins+=price;behavior.purchases.push({nodeId:currentStageNode()?.id,profileId:currentShopProfileId(),offerId:offer.offerId,itemId:item.id,itemType:item.itemType,effectType:item.effect.type,targetId,price,outcome});if(['ADD_CARD','UPGRADE_CARD','REMOVE_CARD'].includes(item.effect.type))behavior.deckChanges++;targetRawEvent('SHOP_PURCHASE',{profileId:currentShopProfileId(),offerId:offer.offerId,itemId:item.id,itemType:item.itemType,effectType:item.effect.type,targetId,price,outcome})}
function completeShopPurchase(item,applyEffect,{targetId=null,price=shopItemPrice(item,targetId)}={}){
  const session=ensureShopSession(),offer=shopOffer(session,item.id),purchased=(session.purchasedItemIds||[]).includes(item.id),effectiveItem={...item,price},availability=globalThis.ShopRuntime.purchaseAvailability({item:effectiveItem,coins,purchaseCount:purchased?item.purchaseLimit:offer?.purchaseCount||0});if(!offer||!availability.allowed){$('#shop-note').textContent=availability.reason==='INSUFFICIENT_COINS'?'购买失败：金币不足。':'购买失败：本商品已达到购买上限。';renderShop();return false}
  const effectId=`SHOP_PURCHASE:${currentStageNode()?.id}:${item.id}`,once=globalThis.runController.runOnce(effectId,()=>{const outcome=applyEffect();coins-=price;const purchasedItemIds=[...new Set([...(session.purchasedItemIds||[]),item.id])],nextSession={...session,purchasedItemIds,selectedItemId:item.id,offers:session.offers.map(entry=>entry.offerId===offer.offerId?{...entry,purchaseCount:entry.purchaseCount+1}:entry)};globalThis.runController.setNodeRuntime({shopSession:nextSession});recordShopPurchase(item,offer,outcome,price,targetId);return outcome});
  if(!once.executed)return false;$('#shop-note').textContent=`购买成功：${once.value}`;window.gameSfx?.('buy');renderShop();window.commitRunSave?.('shop','shop_purchase');return true
}
function shopUpgradeTargets(item){
  if(item.effect.type==='UPGRADE_PERSONA_MAIN')return shopPersonaEntries().filter(({instance,template})=>instance&&(template.effects||[]).some(effect=>['ADD_CHIPS','ADD_MULT','ADD_XMULT_RATE','MULTIPLY_FINAL'].includes(effect.type))).map(({instance,template})=>({id:instance.instanceId,name:template.displayId||template.name,detail:template.mainEffect?.effectText||'人格主词条',level:shopUpgradeTargetLevel(item,instance.instanceId)}));
  if(item.effect.type==='UPGRADE_SUIT')return[{id:'♠',name:'黑桃',detail:'所有黑桃计分牌'},{id:'♥',name:'红桃',detail:'所有红桃计分牌'},{id:'♦',name:'方片',detail:'所有方片计分牌'},{id:'♣',name:'梅花',detail:'所有梅花计分牌'}].map(target=>({...target,level:shopUpgradeTargetLevel(item,target.id)}));
  if(item.effect.type==='UPGRADE_HAND_TYPE')return[...currentHandTypes()].sort((a,b)=>a.displayOrder-b.displayOrder).map(handType=>({id:handType.id,name:handType.name,detail:`基础 ${handType.chips} 筹码 × ${handType.mult} 倍率`,level:shopUpgradeTargetLevel(item,handType.id)}));
  return[];
}
function renderShopUpgradeTargets(){const item=shopItemById.get(shopUpgradeItemId),targets=item?shopUpgradeTargets(item):[];$('#shop-upgrade-title').textContent=item?.name||'选择强化目标';$('#shop-upgrade-copy').textContent=item?globalThis.ShopRuntime.describeEffect(item):'';$('#shop-upgrade-options').innerHTML=targets.map(target=>`<button type="button" class="shop-upgrade-option ${target.id===shopUpgradeTargetId?'selected':''}" data-shop-upgrade-target="${escapePersonaUiText(target.id)}" role="option" aria-selected="${target.id===shopUpgradeTargetId}"><b>${escapePersonaUiText(target.name)}</b><span>${escapePersonaUiText(target.detail)}</span><em>Lv.${target.level}</em></button>`).join('')||'<p>当前没有可强化的目标。</p>';document.querySelectorAll('[data-shop-upgrade-target]').forEach(button=>button.onclick=()=>{shopUpgradeTargetId=button.dataset.shopUpgradeTarget;renderShopUpgradeTargets();window.gameSfx?.('select')});const price=shopUpgradeTargetId?shopItemPrice(item,shopUpgradeTargetId):null;$('#shop-upgrade-price').textContent=price==null?'请选择目标':`本次价格：${price} 金币`;$('#shop-upgrade-confirm').disabled=!shopUpgradeTargetId||coins<price;$('#shop-upgrade-confirm').textContent=shopUpgradeTargetId&&coins<price?'金币不足':'确认购买'}
function openShopUpgradeTarget(item){shopUpgradeItemId=item.id;shopUpgradeTargetId=null;if($('#shop-dialog').open)$('#shop-dialog').close();renderShopUpgradeTargets();$('#shop-upgrade-dialog').showModal();window.commitRunSave?.('shop','shop_upgrade_target_select')}
function closeShopUpgradeTarget(note='已取消选择，未扣除金币。'){if($('#shop-upgrade-dialog').open)$('#shop-upgrade-dialog').close();shopUpgradeItemId=null;shopUpgradeTargetId=null;shopTab='goods';setShopTab('goods');if(!$('#shop-dialog').open)$('#shop-dialog').showModal();$('#shop-note').textContent=note;window.commitRunSave?.('shop','shop_upgrade_target_closed')}
function confirmShopUpgradeTarget(){const item=shopItemById.get(shopUpgradeItemId),targetId=shopUpgradeTargetId;if(!item||!targetId)return;const price=shopItemPrice(item,targetId),purchased=completeShopPurchase(item,()=>{if(item.effect.type==='UPGRADE_PERSONA_MAIN'){const result=personaRuntime.applyPersonaMainUpgrade(targetId,item),template=personaRuntime.getTemplate(result.templateId);return`${template.displayId||template.name} 主词条强化至 Lv.${result.level}。`}const current=currentShopGrowthState();if(item.effect.type==='UPGRADE_SUIT'){const next=globalThis.ShopRuntime.applySuitUpgrade(current,targetId,item);globalThis.runController.setShopGrowthState(next);return`${targetId} 花色强化至 Lv.${next.suitLevelsBySuit[targetId]}。`}const next=globalThis.ShopRuntime.applyHandTypeUpgrade(current,targetId,item);globalThis.runController.setShopGrowthState(next);const handType=currentHandTypeById()[targetId];return`${handType?.name||targetId}强化至 Lv.${next.handTypeLevelsById[targetId]}。`},{targetId,price});if(purchased)closeShopUpgradeTarget($('#shop-note').textContent)}
function buyShopItem(itemId){
  const item=shopItemById.get(itemId),session=ensureShopSession(),offer=shopOffer(session,itemId),purchased=(session.purchasedItemIds||[]).includes(itemId),availability=globalThis.ShopRuntime.purchaseAvailability({item,coins,purchaseCount:purchased?item?.purchaseLimit:offer?.purchaseCount||0});
  if(!item||!offer||!availability.allowed){$('#shop-note').textContent=availability.reason==='INSUFFICIENT_COINS'?'购买失败：金币不足。':'购买失败：本商品已达到购买上限。';renderShop();return}
  if(['UPGRADE_PERSONA_MAIN','UPGRADE_SUIT','UPGRADE_HAND_TYPE'].includes(item.effect.type)){openShopUpgradeTarget(item);return}
  if(item.effect.requiresTarget){deckShopItemId=item.id;if($('#shop-dialog').open)$('#shop-dialog').close();openDeckDialog(false);window.commitRunSave?.('shop','shop_target_select');return}
  if(item.effect.type==='ADD_CARD')completeShopPurchase(item,()=>{const card=globalThis.ShopRuntime.createCardFromItem(item,{instanceKey:`${currentStageNode()?.id}-${offer.offerId}-${offer.purchaseCount+1}`});runDeck.push(card);return`${card.r}${card.s} 已加入本局牌库。`});
  else if(item.effect.type==='ADD_PERSONA')completeShopPurchase(item,()=>{const instance=personaRuntime.createInstance(item.effect.personaTemplateId,{source:'SHOP_PERSONA',generatedAtNodeId:currentStageNode()?.id,generatedAtBattleIndexCompat:battleIndex});const template=personaRuntime.getTemplate(instance.templateId);return`${template.name} 已加入本局人格池（未自动装备）。`});
}
function completeRouteNode(routeResult={}){if($('#shop-dialog').open)$('#shop-dialog').close();if($('#event-dialog').open)$('#event-dialog').close();const node=currentStageNode(),pendingGrowthResult=globalThis.runController.getCurrentRuntime()?.data?.pendingGrowthResult;if(isTargetRun()&&node?.type==='PERSONA_GROWTH'&&pendingGrowthResult){globalThis.runController.completeNode(pendingGrowthResult);return}if(node?.type==='SHOP'){globalThis.runController.completeNode({type:'SHOP_COMPLETED',nodeId:node.id,routeResult});return}globalThis.runController.completeNode({type:'ROUTE_COMPLETED',nodeId:node?.id,routeResult})}
function initializeBattleNode(){const runState=globalThis.runController.getState(),nextIndex=runState.battleIndexCompat,actionRules=currentRunTemplate()?.actionRules||battleConfig;ensurePersonaRuntimeInitialized();personaRuntime.resetBattle();battleIndex=nextIndex;ensureStageLimit();battlePlayed=0;selectedShopItemId=null;deckShopItemId=null;deckShopReturnNote=null;shopUpgradeItemId=null;shopUpgradeTargetId=null;currentRouteNodes=[];currentRandomEvent=null;score=0;const entryBonuses=personaRuntime.getBattleEntryBonuses();hands=(actionRules.baseHands??battleConfig.baseHands)+extraHand+entryBonuses.hands;extraHand=0;discards=(actionRules.baseDiscards??battleConfig.baseDiscards)+extraDiscard+entryBonuses.discards;extraDiscard=0;const entry=stageLimitRuntime?.adjustEntry(activeStageLimit(),{hands,discards,handSize:actionRules.startingHandSize??battleConfig.startingHandSize})||{hands,discards};hands=entry.hands??hands;discards=entry.discards??discards;earnedThisBattle=0;cardGoldThisBattle=0;personaGoldThisBattle=0;inputLocked=true;currentEncounter=null;lastHandType=null;lastHandTypeId=null;setupBattleDeck();if($('#shop-dialog').open)$('#shop-dialog').close();if($('#shop-upgrade-dialog').open)$('#shop-upgrade-dialog').close();if($('#event-dialog').open)$('#event-dialog').close();document.querySelectorAll('[data-route-node]').forEach(button=>button.disabled=false);window.showBattleFromRoute?.();$('#message').innerHTML='<span></span>新牌库已洗牌，等待开始战斗。';document.querySelector('.battle-stage').classList.add('node-flash');setTimeout(()=>document.querySelector('.battle-stage').classList.remove('node-flash'),750);renderConfiguredUI();render();window.openStageIntro?.()}
function personaDetailData(persona){const template=persona.collectionTemplate||runManifest.basePersonas.templates.find(item=>item.id===persona.templateId)||persona,presentation=shopPersonaPresentation(template,null);return{template,presentation}}
function openPersonaDetail(id){const persona=personaPool.find(item=>item.id===id);if(!persona)return false;const {template,presentation}=personaDetailData(persona),art=$('#persona-detail-art');art.style.setProperty('--portrait',persona.portrait?`url('${persona.portrait}')`:'none');$('#persona-detail-id').textContent=template.displayId||'人格牌';$('#persona-detail-name').textContent=template.name||persona.name;$('#persona-detail-trigger').textContent=presentation.triggerText;$('#persona-detail-effect').textContent=presentation.effectText;$('#persona-detail-affixes').innerHTML=presentation.hasAffixes?`<ol class="persona-affix-list">${personaAffixMarkup(template,null)}</ol>`:'';const dialog=$('#persona-detail-dialog');if(!dialog.open)dialog.showModal();window.gameSfx?.('select');return true}
function portraitOnlyCard(persona,className,dataAttribute,extraClass=''){const name=escapePersonaUiText(persona.displayId||persona.name),artClass=persona.portrait?'':'no-art';return`<button type="button" class="${className} ${extraClass}" ${dataAttribute} aria-label="查看${name}详情"><span class="${className}-art ${artClass}" ${persona.portrait?`style="--portrait:url('${persona.portrait}')"`:''}></span></button>`}
function personaInformationCard(persona,className,dataAttribute,extraClass=''){const {template,presentation}=personaDetailData(persona),rawName=template.name||persona.name,rawDisplayId=template.displayId||persona.displayId||'人格牌',name=escapePersonaUiText(rawName),displayId=rawDisplayId===rawName?'':`<small>${escapePersonaUiText(rawDisplayId)}</small>`,trigger=escapePersonaUiText(presentation.triggerText),effect=escapePersonaUiText(presentation.effectText),artClass=persona.portrait?'':'no-art';return`<button type="button" class="${className} persona-information-card ${extraClass}" ${dataAttribute} aria-label="查看${name}详情"><span class="${className}-art persona-information-art ${artClass}" ${persona.portrait?`style="--portrait:url('${persona.portrait}')"`:''}></span><span class="persona-card-name">${displayId}<strong>${name}</strong></span><span class="persona-card-rules"><span class="persona-card-trigger"><i>触发</i><b>${trigger}</b></span><span class="persona-card-effect"><i>效果</i><b>${effect}</b></span></span></button>`}
function renderPersonaLibrary(){syncPermanentPersonaPool();const visible=[...personaPool].sort(comparePersonaLibraryOrder);$('#persona-library').innerHTML=visible.length?visible.map(persona=>portraitOnlyCard(persona,'gallery-card',`data-gallery-persona-id="${persona.id}"`)).join(''):`<p class="library-empty">尚未获得人格牌</p>`;document.querySelectorAll('[data-gallery-persona-id]').forEach(button=>button.onclick=()=>openPersonaDetail(button.dataset.galleryPersonaId))}
window.openPersonaLibrary=()=>{const dialog=$('#persona-dialog');syncPermanentPersonaPool();if(!dialog||!personaPool.length)throw new Error('人格图鉴数据尚未完成加载');renderPersonaLibrary();if(!dialog.open)dialog.showModal();return true};
function placePendingPersona(id,slotIndex){if(!personaPool.some(persona=>persona.id===id)||slotIndex<0||slotIndex>=battleConfig.personaSlots)return;const oldIndex=pendingLoadoutIds.indexOf(id),replaced=pendingLoadoutIds[slotIndex]||null;if(oldIndex>=0)pendingLoadoutIds[oldIndex]=replaced;pendingLoadoutIds[slotIndex]=id;renderStartLoadout();window.gameSfx?.('select')}
function removePendingPersona(slotIndex){pendingLoadoutIds[slotIndex]=null;renderStartLoadout();window.gameSfx?.('discard')}
function renderStartLoadout(){syncPermanentPersonaPool();const seen=new Set();pendingLoadoutIds=new Array(battleConfig.personaSlots).fill(null).map((_,index)=>{const id=pendingLoadoutIds[index];if(!id||seen.has(id)||!personaPool.some(persona=>persona.id===id))return null;seen.add(id);return id});const selectedCount=pendingLoadoutIds.filter(Boolean).length,cards=[...personaPool].sort(comparePersonaLibraryOrder);$('#start-loadout-count').textContent=`${selectedCount} / ${battleConfig.personaSlots}`;$('#start-loadout-slots').innerHTML=new Array(battleConfig.personaSlots).fill(null).map((_,index)=>{const id=pendingLoadoutIds[index],persona=personaPool.find(item=>item.id===id);return`<article class="start-loadout-slot ${persona?'filled':''}" data-loadout-slot="${index}" tabindex="0"><span class="start-loadout-slot-index">${index+1}</span>${persona?`<span class="start-loadout-slot-art ${persona.portrait?'':'no-art'}" draggable="true" data-drag-persona-id="${persona.id}" ${persona.portrait?`style="--portrait:url('${persona.portrait}')"`:''}></span><span class="start-loadout-slot-copy"><b>${escapePersonaUiText(persona.displayId||persona.name)}</b><small>拖动可更换槽位</small></span><button type="button" class="start-loadout-remove" data-remove-loadout-slot="${index}" aria-label="移除${escapePersonaUiText(persona.name)}">×</button>`:'<span class="start-loadout-slot-copy"><b>空槽位</b><small>将人格牌拖到这里</small></span>'}</article>`}).join('');$('#start-loadout-library').innerHTML=cards.map(persona=>personaInformationCard(persona,'start-loadout-persona',`data-loadout-persona-id="${persona.id}" draggable="true"`,pendingLoadoutIds.includes(persona.id)?'equipped':'')).join('');const ready=selectedCount===battleConfig.personaSlots;$('#start-loadout-confirm').disabled=!ready;$('#start-loadout-note').textContent=ready?'阵容已就绪。你仍可拖动调整槽位顺序。':`还需要装备 ${battleConfig.personaSlots-selectedCount} 张人格牌。`;
  document.querySelectorAll('[data-loadout-persona-id]').forEach(button=>{button.onclick=()=>openPersonaDetail(button.dataset.loadoutPersonaId);button.ondragstart=event=>{draggedPersonaId=button.dataset.loadoutPersonaId;event.dataTransfer?.setData('text/persona-id',draggedPersonaId)}});document.querySelectorAll('[data-drag-persona-id]').forEach(art=>art.ondragstart=event=>{draggedPersonaId=art.dataset.dragPersonaId;event.dataTransfer?.setData('text/persona-id',draggedPersonaId)});document.querySelectorAll('[data-loadout-slot]').forEach(slot=>{slot.ondragover=event=>{event.preventDefault();slot.classList.add('drag-over')};slot.ondragleave=()=>slot.classList.remove('drag-over');slot.ondrop=event=>{event.preventDefault();slot.classList.remove('drag-over');const id=event.dataTransfer?.getData('text/persona-id')||draggedPersonaId;if(id)placePendingPersona(id,Number(slot.dataset.loadoutSlot));draggedPersonaId=null}});document.querySelectorAll('[data-remove-loadout-slot]').forEach(button=>button.onclick=event=>{event.stopPropagation();removePendingPersona(Number(button.dataset.removeLoadoutSlot))})}
window.openStartPersonaLoadout=()=>{syncPermanentPersonaPool();const selected=normalizedGalleryLoadout(equippedPersonaIds);pendingLoadoutIds=new Array(battleConfig.personaSlots).fill(null).map((_,index)=>selected[index]||null);renderStartLoadout();const dialog=$('#start-loadout-dialog');if(!dialog.open)dialog.showModal();return true};
function confirmStartPersonaLoadout(){const selected=pendingLoadoutIds.filter(Boolean);if(selected.length!==battleConfig.personaSlots)return false;equippedPersonaIds=[...selected];try{localStorage.setItem('persona-loadout',JSON.stringify(equippedPersonaIds))}catch{}$('#start-loadout-dialog').close();window.confirmStartRunWithLoadout?.();return true}
function saveForgedPersonas(){try{localStorage.setItem('persona-forged-instances',JSON.stringify(personaPool.filter(persona=>Number.isInteger(persona.roll))))}catch{}}
function deckCardsForTab(){if(deckShopItemId)return runDeck;return deckTab==='used'?usedCards:deckTab==='discarded'?discardedCards:[...hand,...deck]}
function sortedDeckCards(cards){return globalThis.DeckSortRuntime.sortCards(cards,deckSortMode)}
function setDeckSort(mode){if(!['rank','suit'].includes(mode))return;deckSortMode=mode;renderDeckDialog();window.gameSfx?.('button')}
function cardUpgradeSummary(card){const attributes=globalThis.ShopRuntime.cardUpgradeAttributes(card),parts=[];if(attributes.bonusChips)parts.push(`筹+${attributes.bonusChips}`);if(attributes.bonusCoins)parts.push(`金+${attributes.bonusCoins}`);if(attributes.bonusMult)parts.push(`倍+${attributes.bonusMult}`);if(attributes.bonusXmultRate)parts.push(`独+${Number((attributes.bonusXmultRate*100).toFixed(4))}%`);return parts.join(' · ')}
function deckSuitGrowth(card){return buildInspection.suitView(card,currentShopGrowthState())}
function renderDeckGrowthSummary(){const summary=$('#deck-growth-summary'),view=buildInspection.suitSummary(currentShopGrowthState());summary.classList.toggle('hidden',!view.labels.length);summary.innerHTML=view.markup;summary.setAttribute('aria-label',view.ariaLabel)}
function renderDeckDialog(){
  const shopTarget=!!deckShopItemId,targetEnabled=shopTarget||balance.featureFlags.manualDeckTargetBoostEnabled;if(shopTarget){deckTab='deck';deckTargetMode=true}else if(!targetEnabled){deckTargetMode=false;deckSelectedUid=null}
  document.querySelectorAll('.debug-deck-target').forEach(element=>element.classList.toggle('hidden',!targetEnabled));document.querySelector('#deck-dialog>h2').textContent=shopTarget?'选择商品作用的卡牌':'牌库查看';
  if(!deckSelectedUid){$('#deck-detail-card').classList.remove('has-card-art');$('#deck-detail-card').textContent='—';$('#deck-detail-name').textContent='尚未选择';$('#deck-detail-desc').textContent=shopTarget?'请选择一张牌作为商品作用目标。':'选择一张牌查看详情。'}
  const cards=sortedDeckCards(deckCardsForTab());document.querySelectorAll('[data-deck-tab]').forEach(button=>{button.classList.toggle('active',button.dataset.deckTab===deckTab);button.disabled=shopTarget&&button.dataset.deckTab!=='deck'});$('#deck-total').textContent=cards.length;$('#deck-selectable').textContent=deckTab==='deck'?cards.length:0;renderDeckGrowthSummary();$('#deck-target-mode').textContent=shopTarget?'请选择 1 张目标牌':deckTargetMode?'取消目标选择':'选择强化目标';$('#deck-target-mode').disabled=shopTarget;$('#deck-done').textContent=shopTarget?'取消购买':'关闭';
  $('#deck-sort-rank').classList.toggle('active',deckSortMode==='rank');$('#deck-sort-rank').setAttribute('aria-pressed',deckSortMode==='rank');$('#deck-sort-suit').classList.toggle('active',deckSortMode==='suit');$('#deck-sort-suit').setAttribute('aria-pressed',deckSortMode==='suit');
  $('#deck-grid').innerHTML=cards.map(card=>{const summary=cardUpgradeSummary(card),upgradeStrip=cardPresentation.upgradeStrip(card),suitGrowth=deckSuitGrowth(card),art=cardArtImage(card,'deck-card-art'),fallback=art?'':`<b>${card.r}</b><span>${card.s}</span>`,growthLabel=suitGrowth.bonus?`${card.s}花色强化，每次作为计分牌增加${formatGrowthNumber(suitGrowth.bonus)}筹码`:'';return`<button class="deck-item ${cardArtClass(card)} ${card.c} ${suitGrowth.bonus?'suit-enhanced':''} ${deckSelectedUid===card.uid?'selected':''}" data-deck-uid="${escapePersonaUiText(card.uid)}" aria-label="${escapePersonaUiText(`${card.r}${card.s}${growthLabel?`，${growthLabel}`:''}${summary?`，卡牌强化${summary}`:''}`)}" ${deckTab!=='deck'||!deckTargetMode?'disabled':''}>${art}${fallback}${suitGrowth.bonus?`<strong class="deck-suit-bonus" title="${escapePersonaUiText(growthLabel)}">+${formatGrowthNumber(suitGrowth.bonus)}</strong>`:''}${upgradeStrip}</button>`}).join('')||'<p class="deck-empty">这里暂时没有牌。</p>';
  document.querySelectorAll('[data-deck-uid]').forEach(button=>button.onclick=()=>selectDeckTarget(button.dataset.deckUid));$('#deck-confirm').disabled=!deckTargetMode||!deckSelectedUid;$('#deck-confirm').textContent=shopTarget?'确认购买并应用':'确认选择';
}
function openDeckDialog(returnToReport=false){deckReturnToReport=returnToReport;deckShopReturnNote=null;deckTab='deck';deckTargetMode=!!deckShopItemId;deckSelectedUid=null;if(returnToReport&&$('#report-dialog').open)$('#report-dialog').close();renderDeckDialog();$('#deck-dialog').showModal()}
function selectDeckTarget(uid){const shopTarget=!!deckShopItemId;if((!shopTarget&&!balance.featureFlags.manualDeckTargetBoostEnabled)||!deckTargetMode)return;deckSelectedUid=uid;const card=deckCardsForTab().find(item=>item.uid===uid),item=shopTarget?shopItemById.get(deckShopItemId):null,value=balance.featureFlags.manualDeckTargetBoostValue,detail=$('#deck-detail-card'),art=card?cardArtImage(card,'deck-card-art'):'';detail.classList.toggle('has-card-art',!!art);detail.innerHTML=card?(art||`${card.s} ${card.r}`):'—';$('#deck-detail-name').textContent=card?`${card.r}${card.s} · ${shopTarget?item?.name:'强化目标'}`:'尚未选择';$('#deck-detail-desc').textContent=card?(shopTarget?`${globalThis.ShopRuntime.describeEffect(item)} 当前强化：${cardUpgradeSummary(card)||'无'}。`:`确认后，该牌在本局每次计分时提供 +${value} 基础筹码。`):'选择一张牌。';renderDeckDialog()}
function confirmDeckTarget(){
  if(deckShopItemId){
    const item=shopItemById.get(deckShopItemId),card=runDeck.find(entry=>entry.uid===deckSelectedUid);if(!item||!card)return;
    const purchased=completeShopPurchase(item,()=>{const index=runDeck.findIndex(entry=>entry.uid===card.uid);if(index<0)throw new Error('所选卡牌已不在本局牌库');if(item.effect.type==='REMOVE_CARD'){const [removed]=runDeck.splice(index,1);return`${removed.r}${removed.s} 已从本局牌库移除。`}const updated=globalThis.ShopRuntime.applyCardUpgrade(runDeck[index],item);runDeck[index]=updated;return`${updated.r}${updated.s} 已完成${item.name}。`},{targetId:card.uid});
    if(purchased){deckShopReturnNote=$('#shop-note').textContent;closeDeckDialog()}return;
  }
  if(!balance.featureFlags.manualDeckTargetBoostEnabled)return;const card=[...hand,...deck].find(item=>item.uid===deckSelectedUid),value=balance.featureFlags.manualDeckTargetBoostValue;if(card){card.bonus=(card.bonus||0)+value;const template=runDeck.find(item=>item.templateId===card.templateId);if(template)template.bonus=(template.bonus||0)+value;$('#message').innerHTML=`<span></span>${card.r}${card.s} 已获得 +${value} 计分筹码，后续战斗保留。`;window.gameSfx?.('forge')}closeDeckDialog();render()
}
function closeDeckDialog(){const returningToShop=!!deckShopItemId,note=deckShopReturnNote;if($('#deck-dialog').open)$('#deck-dialog').close();if(returningToShop){deckShopItemId=null;deckShopReturnNote=null;deckSelectedUid=null;deckTargetMode=false;shopTab='goods';setShopTab('goods');if(!$('#shop-dialog').open)$('#shop-dialog').showModal();$('#shop-note').textContent=note||'已取消选择，未扣除金币。';window.commitRunSave?.('shop',note?'shop_purchase_returned':'shop_target_cancelled');return}if(deckReturnToReport){deckReturnToReport=false;$('#report-dialog').showModal()}}
function openReport(persist=true){
  currentPersonaReport=buildPersonaReport();const report=currentPersonaReport;$('#report-title').textContent=report.title;$('#report-tags').innerHTML=report.tags.map(tag=>`<span>${tag}</span>`).join('');$('#report-copy').textContent=report.copy;
  $('#report-metrics').innerHTML=report.metricDefs.map(([key,label])=>`<div class="report-metric"><span>${label}</span><i style="--value:${report.metrics[key]}%"></i><b>${report.metrics[key]}%</b></div>`).join('');$('#report-evidence').innerHTML=report.evidence.map(text=>`<li>${text}</li>`).join('');$('#report-dialog').showModal();if(persist)window.commitRunSave?.('report','report_opened');window.gameSfx?.('win')
}
const forgeModes=['映照','偏转','裂变'];
const forgePools=[['observer','collector','restraint','commitment'],['wanderer','resonance','commitment'],['pathfinder','purger','collector']];
function personaBehaviorMatch(persona,modeIndex){
  const m=currentPersonaReport?.metrics||{},dominant=currentPersonaReport?.dominantType||'高牌',structured=['对子','两对','三条','葫芦','四条'].includes(dominant),scores={observer:(dominant.includes('顺')?75:25)+(m.focus||0)*.2,collector:(structured?70:25)+(m.focus||0)*.2,restraint:(dominant==='高牌'?70:20)+(m.reserve||0)*.25,commitment:(m.overkill||0)*.45+(100-(m.reserve||0))*.35+20,wanderer:(m.diversity||0)*.55+(m.adaptation||0)*.35+10,resonance:(m.suit||0)*.7+20,pathfinder:(m.volatility||0)*.5+(100-(m.reserve||0))*.25+(currentPersonaReport?.forgeBias.risk||0)*.25,purger:(m.discard||0)*.55+(m.volatility||0)*.25+20};
  let match=scores[persona.id]||30;if(modeIndex===1)match=match*.65+(100-(m.focus||0))*.35;if(modeIndex===2)match=match*.55+(currentPersonaReport?.forgeBias.risk||0)*.45;return pct(match)
}
function forgeTier(roll){return roll===20?'完美闪光':roll>=16?'闪光':roll>=11?'高阶':roll>=6?'强化':'基础'}
function forgeValue(effectType,roll){const index=roll<=5?0:roll<=10?1:roll<=15?2:3,values={ADD_CHIPS:[20,35,55,80],ADD_MULT:[2,4,7,10],MULTIPLY_FINAL:[1.1,1.2,1.35,1.5]};return values[normalizedPersonaEffectType(effectType)][index]}
function forgePersonaInstance(template,roll,modeIndex,match){
  const effectType=normalizedPersonaEffectType(template.effectType),value=forgeValue(effectType,roll),tier=forgeTier(roll),persona={...template,effectType,value},valueText=personaValueText(persona),triggerText=personaTriggerTextFromConfig(persona),perfectEcho=roll===20,baseId=template.baseId||template.id;
  return{...persona,id:`forge-${Date.now()}-${modeIndex}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,7)}`,baseId,mode:forgeModes[modeIndex],code:`D20_${roll}_${baseId.toUpperCase()}`,tier,roll,match,perfectEcho,effect:`${triggerText} ${valueText}`,desc:`${triggerText}，${valueText}。${perfectEcho?'本场首次成功触发时额外回响一次。':''}`}
}
function recommendedForgeIndex(){return forgeResults.reduce((best,result,index)=>result.match>forgeResults[best].match?index:best,0)}
function renderForge(){
  document.querySelectorAll('[data-forge-tab]').forEach((tab,index)=>{tab.classList.toggle('active',index===forgeStep&&forgeStep<3);tab.classList.toggle('done',index<forgeStep)});
  $('#forge-generated').innerHTML=new Array(3).fill(null).map((_,index)=>{const result=forgeResults[index];return result?`<div class="forge-slot filled"><small>${forgeModes[index]} · 二十面骰 ${result.roll} 点 · ${result.persona.tier}</small><b>${result.persona.name}</b><span>${result.persona.effect}</span></div>`:`<div class="forge-slot"><small>${forgeModes[index]}</small><b>＋</b><span>等待生成</span></div>`}).join('');
  const next=$('#forge-next');next.disabled=false;next.querySelector('span').textContent=forgeStep<3?'投掷二十面骰':'进入三选一';
}
function setPersonaPortrait(element,persona){element.classList.toggle('has-art',Boolean(persona?.portrait));if(persona?.portrait)element.style.setProperty('--portrait',`url('${persona.portrait}')`);else element.style.removeProperty('--portrait')}
function openForge(persist=true,restoring=false){if(!currentPersonaReport)currentPersonaReport=buildPersonaReport();if(!restoring){forgeStep=0;forgeResults=[]}$('#summary').textContent=`画像倾向：${currentPersonaReport.tags.join('、')}。二十面骰决定强度，行为匹配决定机制候选。`;$('#forge-roll-value').textContent=forgeResults.at(-1)?.roll??'?';$('#forge-point').textContent=forgeResults.at(-1)?.roll??'—';$('#forge-tier').textContent=forgeResults.at(-1)?.persona?.tier??'等待投掷';$('#forge-result-name').textContent=forgeResults.at(-1)?.persona?.name??'尚未生成';$('#forge-result-desc').textContent=forgeResults.at(-1)?.persona?.desc??'投掷二十面骰，生成符合本局行为画像的人格牌。';setPersonaPortrait(document.querySelector('.forge-avatar'),forgeResults.at(-1)?.persona||null);renderForge();$('#forge-dialog').showModal();if(persist)window.commitRunSave?.('forge','forge_opened');window.gameSfx?.('forge')}
async function rollForge(){
  if(forgeStep>=3)return;const step=forgeStep,dice=document.querySelector('.d20-shape'),roll=Math.floor(Math.random()*20)+1,pool=forgePools[step].map(id=>personaPool.find(p=>p.id===id)).filter(Boolean),used=new Set(forgeResults.map(result=>result.persona.baseId));let candidates=pool.filter(persona=>!used.has(persona.baseId||persona.id));if(!candidates.length)candidates=personaPool.filter(persona=>!persona.roll&&!used.has(persona.baseId||persona.id));candidates.sort((a,b)=>personaBehaviorMatch(b,step)-personaBehaviorMatch(a,step));const template=candidates[0],match=personaBehaviorMatch(template,step),persona=forgePersonaInstance(template,roll,step,match);
  $('#forge-next').disabled=true;dice.classList.remove('rolling');void dice.offsetWidth;dice.classList.add('rolling');window.gameSfx?.('select');await wait(650);$('#forge-roll-value').textContent=roll;$('#forge-point').textContent=roll;$('#forge-tier').textContent=forgeTier(roll);$('#forge-result-name').textContent=persona.name;$('#forge-result-desc').textContent=persona.desc;setPersonaPortrait(document.querySelector('.forge-avatar'),persona);forgeResults.push({roll,persona,match});forgeStep++;globalThis.runController?.setNodeRuntime({forgeStep,forgeResults});renderForge();window.commitRunSave?.('forge','forge_candidate_generated');
}
function forgeNextAction(){forgeStep<3?rollForge():openPersonaChoice()}
function renderPersonaChoice(){
  const recommended=recommendedForgeIndex();$('#choice-cards').innerHTML=forgeResults.map((result,index)=>`<button class="choice-card ${index===recommended?'recommended':''} ${index===choiceSelectedIndex?'selected':''}" data-choice-index="${index}"><div class="choice-portrait ${result.persona.portrait?'has-art':''}" ${result.persona.portrait?`style="--portrait:url('${result.persona.portrait}')"`:''}></div><small>${forgeModes[index]} · 二十面骰 ${result.roll} 点 · 匹配 ${result.match}%</small><h3>${result.persona.name}</h3><div class="acquire-tags"><span>${result.persona.mode}</span><span>${result.persona.effect}</span></div><p>${result.persona.desc}</p></button>`).join('');document.querySelectorAll('[data-choice-index]').forEach(button=>button.onclick=()=>{choiceSelectedIndex=Number(button.dataset.choiceIndex);renderPersonaChoice();window.gameSfx?.('select')});const result=forgeResults[choiceSelectedIndex],isRecommended=choiceSelectedIndex===recommended;$('#choice-name').textContent=result.persona.name;$('#choice-tags').innerHTML=`<span>${result.persona.mode}</span><span>二十面骰 ${result.roll} 点</span><span>匹配 ${result.match}%</span>`;$('#choice-desc').textContent=result.persona.desc;$('#choice-reason').textContent=isRecommended?`本地画像显示“${currentPersonaReport.tags.join('、')}”是本局主要倾向；该候选匹配度最高。`:`该候选匹配度为 ${result.match}%，提供与主要倾向不同的成长方向。`;$('#choice-confirm').disabled=false;
}
function openPersonaChoice(){choiceSelectedIndex=recommendedForgeIndex();$('#forge-dialog').close();renderPersonaChoice();$('#choice-dialog').showModal();window.commitRunSave?.('choice','persona_choice')}
function confirmPersonaChoice(){const result=forgeResults[choiceSelectedIndex];if(!result)return;if(!personaPool.some(persona=>persona.id===result.persona.id))personaPool.push(result.persona);saveForgedPersonas();$('#choice-dialog').close();openAcquire(result.persona)}
function returnToForge(){if($('#choice-dialog').open)$('#choice-dialog').close();$('#forge-dialog').showModal()}
function renderAcquireLoadout(){
  $('#acquire-loadout').innerHTML=new Array(battleConfig.personaSlots).fill(null).map((_,index)=>{const persona=personaPool.find(p=>p.id===equippedPersonaIds[index]);return persona?`<div class="acquire-slot filled"><div class="slot-portrait ${persona.portrait?'has-art':''}" ${persona.portrait?`style="--portrait:url('${persona.portrait}')"`:''}></div><b>${String(index+1).padStart(2,'0')} · ${persona.name}</b><small>${persona.effect}</small></div>`:`<div class="acquire-slot"><b>${String(index+1).padStart(2,'0')}</b><span>＋ 空位</span></div>`}).join('');
}
function openAcquire(persona,persist=true){acquiredPersona=persona;$('#forge-dialog').close();$('#acquire-name').textContent=persona.name;$('#acquire-tags').innerHTML=`<span>${persona.mode}</span><span>二十面骰 ${persona.roll} 点</span><span>${persona.tier}</span>`;$('#acquire-desc').textContent=persona.desc;$('#acquire-detail-name').textContent=persona.name;$('#acquire-detail-desc').textContent=persona.desc;setPersonaPortrait(document.querySelector('.acquire-portrait'),persona);renderAcquireLoadout();$('#acquire-dialog').showModal();if(persist)window.commitRunSave?.('acquire','persona_acquired');window.gameSfx?.('win')}
function finishAcquire(){const dialog=$('#acquire-dialog');if(dialog.open)dialog.close();globalThis.runController.completeNode({type:'FORGE_COMPLETED',nodeId:currentStageNode()?.id,acquiredPersonaId:acquiredPersona?.id||null})}
function equipAcquired(){if(acquiredPersona&&!equippedPersonaIds.includes(acquiredPersona.id)){if(equippedPersonaIds.length<battleConfig.personaSlots)equippedPersonaIds.push(acquiredPersona.id);else equippedPersonaIds[battleConfig.personaSlots-1]=acquiredPersona.id;saveForgedPersonas();try{localStorage.setItem('persona-loadout',JSON.stringify(equippedPersonaIds))}catch{}}finishAcquire()}
function targetEffectCopy(template){if(template.mainEffect?.effectText)return[template.mainEffect.effectText,template.mainEffect.growthText].filter(Boolean).join('；');return(template.effects||[]).map(effect=>effect.type==='ADD_CHIPS'?`+${effect.value??effect.valuePerStack+' /层'} 筹码`:effect.type==='ADD_MULT'?`+${effect.value??effect.valuePerStack+' /层'} 倍率`:effect.type==='ADD_XMULT_RATE'?`+${effect.value??effect.valuePerStack+' /层'} 独立倍率`:effect.type==='MULTIPLY_FINAL'?`最终倍率 ×${effect.value}`:effect.type==='CLEAR_RUNTIME_FLAG'?'触发后清除蓄能':effect.type==='SET_RUNTIME_FLAG'?'建立蓄能状态':effect.type==='ADD_GROWTH_STACK'?'增加成长层数':'状态型效果').join(' · ')||'状态型效果'}
function migrateLegacyTargetLoadout(){const runtimeData=globalThis.runController.getCurrentRuntime()?.data||{},templateIds=normalizedGalleryLoadout(runtimeData.selectedTemplateIds?.length?runtimeData.selectedTemplateIds:equippedPersonaIds);equippedPersonaIds=[...templateIds];personaRuntime.initializeLoadout(templateIds.map(id=>({templateId:id,instanceId:`BASE_INSTANCE_${id}`,source:'BASE_PERSONA'})),{generatedAtNodeId:'TARGET_PERSONA_SELECT',generatedAtBattleIndexCompat:0,source:'BASE_PERSONA'});globalThis.runController.updateRunResult({initialPersonaTemplateIds:[...templateIds]});updateTargetMetrics(metrics=>{metrics.personaEquipHistory.push({nodeId:'TARGET_PERSONA_SELECT',action:'LEGACY_LOADOUT_MIGRATED',equippedPersonaInstanceIds:personaRuntime.getState().equippedPersonaInstanceIds,timestamp:Date.now()});metrics.rawEvents.push({type:'PERSONA_LOADOUT_MIGRATED',nodeId:'TARGET_PERSONA_SELECT',templateIds,timestamp:Date.now()})});globalThis.runController.setNodeRuntime({selectedTemplateIds:[...templateIds],confirmed:true,migratedWithoutUi:true});globalThis.runController.completeNode({type:'PERSONA_LOADOUT_CONFIRMED',nodeId:'TARGET_PERSONA_SELECT',templateIds:[...templateIds]})}
function renderPersonaGrowth(){
  const runtimeState=personaRuntime.getState(),instance=runtimeState.personaInstancesById[currentGrowthInstanceId],template=instance&&personaRuntime.getTemplate(instance.templateId);if(!template)return;
  const feedback=globalThis.PersonaFeedback.cardView(template,instance.runtimeState),equippedIds=runtimeState.equippedPersonaInstanceIds.filter(Boolean),ownedIds=runtimeState.runPersonaPool.filter(id=>runtimeState.personaInstancesById[id]),otherIds=ownedIds.filter(id=>id!==currentGrowthInstanceId&&!equippedIds.includes(id)),mainLabel=template.aiPersonaMeta?'可成长人格':template.mainEntry?`主词条 · ${template.mainEntry}`:`类型 · ${template.mode||'人格'}`,portraitStyle=template.portrait?`style="--portrait:url('${template.portrait}')"`:'';
  $('#persona-growth-owned-count').textContent=`${ownedIds.length} 张`;$('#persona-growth-equipped-count').textContent=`${equippedIds.length} / ${battleConfig.personaSlots}`;
  $('#persona-growth-card').innerHTML=`<article class="growth-new-card"><div class="growth-new-portrait ${template.portrait?'has-art':''}" ${portraitStyle}>◇</div><div class="growth-new-copy"><h3>${escapePersonaUiText(personaDisplayName(template))}</h3><span class="growth-new-main">${escapePersonaUiText(mainLabel)}</span><dl class="growth-new-details"><div><dt>触发条件</dt><dd>${escapePersonaUiText(feedback.trigger)}</dd></div><div><dt>生效效果</dt><dd>${escapePersonaUiText(feedback.reward)}</dd></div>${template.mainEffect?.growthText?`<div><dt>成长方式</dt><dd>${escapePersonaUiText(template.mainEffect.growthText)}</dd></div>`:''}</dl></div></article>`;
  $('#persona-growth-slots').innerHTML=new Array(battleConfig.personaSlots).fill(null).map((_,index)=>{const id=runtimeState.equippedPersonaInstanceIds[index],equipped=id&&runtimeState.personaInstancesById[id],equippedTemplate=equipped&&personaRuntime.getTemplate(equipped.templateId),equippedFeedback=equippedTemplate&&globalThis.PersonaFeedback.cardView(equippedTemplate,equipped.runtimeState),selected=growthSelectedSlot===index,trigger=equippedTemplate&&(equippedTemplate.mainEffect?.triggerText||equippedFeedback?.trigger||'满足人格触发条件时'),effect=equippedTemplate&&(equippedTemplate.mainEffect?.effectText||equippedFeedback?.reward||targetEffectCopy(equippedTemplate));return`<button type="button" data-growth-slot="${index}" class="${selected?'selected':''}" aria-pressed="${selected}"><span class="growth-slot-index">${String(index+1).padStart(2,'0')}</span><span class="growth-slot-copy"><b>${escapePersonaUiText(equippedTemplate?.name||'空槽位')}</b>${equippedTemplate?`<span class="growth-slot-trigger">${escapePersonaUiText(trigger)}</span><small>${escapePersonaUiText(effect)}</small>`:''}</span><span class="growth-slot-action">${selected?(equippedTemplate?'将替换':'将放入'):'选择'}</span></button>`}).join('');
  const otherNames=otherIds.map(id=>{const owned=runtimeState.personaInstancesById[id],ownedTemplate=owned&&personaRuntime.getTemplate(owned.templateId);return ownedTemplate?.name}).filter(Boolean),ownedSummary=$('#persona-growth-owned-summary');ownedSummary.classList.toggle('hidden',!otherNames.length);ownedSummary.innerHTML=otherNames.length?`<b>其他未装备</b>${otherNames.map(name=>`<span>${escapePersonaUiText(name)}</span>`).join('')}`:'';
  const replacedId=runtimeState.equippedPersonaInstanceIds[growthSelectedSlot],replaced=replacedId&&runtimeState.personaInstancesById[replacedId],replacedTemplate=replaced&&personaRuntime.getTemplate(replaced.templateId),slotLabel=`槽位 ${growthSelectedSlot+1}`;$('#persona-growth-replace-note').innerHTML=replacedTemplate?`<strong>${escapePersonaUiText(personaDisplayName(template))}</strong> → ${slotLabel}，替换 <em>${escapePersonaUiText(personaDisplayName(replacedTemplate))}</em>（旧牌保留）`:`<strong>${escapePersonaUiText(personaDisplayName(template))}</strong> → 空的 ${slotLabel}`;
  const diagnostic=$('#persona-growth-ai-status'),generation=globalThis.runController.getCurrentRuntime()?.data?.aiPersonaGeneration;diagnostic.classList.toggle('hidden',!developerModeActive());if(developerModeActive()){const selectedId=generation?.selectedCandidateId?` · 候选 ${generation.selectedCandidateId}`:'';diagnostic.textContent=instance.source==='AI_GENERATED_REMOTE_V1'?`AI调用：DeepSeek 已参与选择${selectedId}`:instance.source==='AI_GENERATED_OFFLINE_DEV'?'AI调用：离线生成测试，未请求 DeepSeek':instance.source==='AI_GENERATED_LOCAL_V1'?`AI调用：已使用本地安全兜底（${generation?.transportStatus||'远程状态未记录'}）`:'AI调用：该人格来自预设配置';}
  $('#persona-growth-confirm').textContent=replacedTemplate?`替换 ${replacedTemplate.name} 并继续`:`装备至 ${slotLabel} 并继续`;
  document.querySelectorAll('[data-growth-slot]').forEach(button=>button.onclick=()=>{growthSelectedSlot=Number(button.dataset.growthSlot);renderPersonaGrowth();window.gameSfx?.('select')});$('#persona-growth-keep').classList.toggle('hidden',!isTargetRun())
}
function finishPersonaGrowthOpen(instance,node,profile,persist,restoring,recordGeneration=true){
  if(!instance){instance=personaRuntime.createInstance(profile.templateId,{source:profile.source,generatedAtNodeId:node.id,generatedAtBattleIndexCompat:globalThis.runController.getState().battleIndexCompat});globalThis.runController.setNodeRuntime({generatedPersonaInstanceId:instance.instanceId})}
  currentGrowthInstanceId=instance.instanceId;if(recordGeneration)updateTargetMetrics(metrics=>{if(!metrics.generatedPersonaInstanceIds.includes(instance.instanceId))metrics.generatedPersonaInstanceIds.push(instance.instanceId);const eventType=instance.source==='AI_GENERATED_OFFLINE_DEV'?'OFFLINE_AI_PERSONA_GENERATED':instance.source==='AI_GENERATED_REMOTE_V1'?'REMOTE_AI_PERSONA_GENERATED':instance.source==='AI_GENERATED_LOCAL_V1'?'LOCAL_AI_PERSONA_GENERATED':'PERSONA_GENERATED',generation=globalThis.runController.getCurrentRuntime()?.data?.aiPersonaGeneration,generatedTemplate=personaRuntime.getTemplate(instance.templateId),meta=generatedTemplate?.aiPersonaMeta||{};metrics.rawEvents.push({type:eventType,nodeId:node.id,instanceId:instance.instanceId,templateId:instance.templateId,selectionSource:generation?.selectionSource||null,transportStatus:generation?.transportStatus||null,selectedCandidateId:generation?.selectedCandidateId||null,mechanismFamilyId:meta.mechanismFamilyId||null,triggerPartId:meta.components?.triggerPartId||null,timestamp:Date.now()})});growthSelectedSlot=0;renderPersonaGrowth();const dialog=$('#persona-growth-dialog');if(!dialog.open)dialog.showModal();if(persist)window.commitRunSave?.('persona_growth',restoring?'persona_growth_restored':'persona_growth_generated');return instance;
}
function openPersonaGrowth(persist=true,restoring=false){
  if(personaGrowthGenerationPending)return;const node=currentStageNode(),profile=personaGrowthProfileById.get(node?.growthProfileId);let runtimeData=globalThis.runController.getCurrentRuntime()?.data||{},snapshot=runtimeData.aiBehaviorSnapshot;if(isTargetRun()&&!snapshot){snapshot=buildAiBehaviorSnapshot(node);if(snapshot)runtimeData=globalThis.runController.setNodeRuntime({aiBehaviorSnapshot:snapshot})||runtimeData}currentGrowthInstanceId=runtimeData.generatedPersonaInstanceId||null;if(currentGrowthInstanceId){const instance=personaRuntime.getState().personaInstancesById[currentGrowthInstanceId];if(instance)return finishPersonaGrowthOpen(instance,node,profile,persist,restoring,false)}
  let generated=null;if(aiPersonaGenerationActive()&&snapshot)generated=generateAiPersonaV1(node,profile,snapshot);if(generated&&typeof generated.then==='function'){personaGrowthGenerationPending=true;generated.then(instance=>finishPersonaGrowthOpen(instance,node,profile,persist,restoring)).catch(()=>finishPersonaGrowthOpen(null,node,profile,persist,restoring)).finally(()=>{personaGrowthGenerationPending=false});return}return finishPersonaGrowthOpen(generated,node,profile,persist,restoring);
}
function completeTargetGrowth(action,slotIndex=null){const node=currentStageNode(),runtime=globalThis.runController.getCurrentRuntime(),result={type:'PERSONA_GROWTH_COMPLETED',nodeId:node.id,instanceId:currentGrowthInstanceId,action,slotIndex};updateTargetMetrics(metrics=>{metrics.growthNodeDurationMs[node.id]=Math.max(0,Date.now()-(runtime?.enteredAt||Date.now()));metrics.personaEquipHistory.push({nodeId:node.id,action,generatedPersonaInstanceId:currentGrowthInstanceId,slotIndex,equippedPersonaInstanceIds:personaRuntime.getState().equippedPersonaInstanceIds,timestamp:Date.now()});metrics.rawEvents.push({type:'PERSONA_GROWTH_DECISION',nodeId:node.id,action,instanceId:currentGrowthInstanceId,slotIndex,timestamp:Date.now()})});$('#persona-growth-dialog').close();if(isTargetRun()){globalThis.runController.setNodeRuntime({pendingGrowthResult:result});window.showBattleFromRoute?.();openShop();return}globalThis.runController.completeNode(result)}

function keepPersonaGrowth(){if(!isTargetRun()||!currentGrowthInstanceId)return;completeTargetGrowth('KEEP',null)}
function buildTargetReport(){const metrics=globalThis.runController.getRunMetrics(),runtimeState=personaRuntime.getState(),battleNodes=currentBattleNodeSequence(),last=metrics.battleScores.at(-1),finalBattleNode=battleNodes.find(node=>node.finalBattle)||battleNodes.at(-1),completed=last?.nodeId===finalBattleNode?.id&&last.win;metrics.totalRunDurationMs=Math.max(0,Date.now()-globalThis.runController.getState().runStartedAt);globalThis.runController.updateRunMetrics(metrics);globalThis.runController.updateRunResult({completed,finalNodeId:last?.nodeId||null});$('#target-report-summary').innerHTML=`<article><small>通关状态</small><strong>${completed?'已通关':'未通关'}</strong></article><article><small>累计得分</small><strong>${metrics.runTotalScore}</strong></article><article><small>最高单手</small><strong>${metrics.maxSingleHandScore}</strong></article><article><small>用时</small><strong>${Math.round(metrics.totalRunDurationMs/1000)} 秒</strong></article>`;$('#target-report-battles').innerHTML=battleNodes.map((node,index)=>{const item=metrics.battleScores.find(entry=>entry.nodeId===node.id);return`<article><b>战斗 ${String(index+1).padStart(2,'0')}</b><span>${item?`${item.score} / ${item.targetScore} · ${item.win?'胜':'败'}`:'未进入'}</span></article>`}).join('');$('#target-report-personas').innerHTML=runtimeState.runPersonaPool.map(id=>{const instance=runtimeState.personaInstancesById[id],template=instance&&personaRuntime.getTemplate(instance.templateId),triggers=metrics.personaTriggersByInstance[id]||0;return`<article><b>${template?personaDisplayName(template):'未命名人格'}</b><small>${runtimeState.equippedPersonaInstanceIds.includes(id)?'当前装备':'未装备'} · 触发 ${triggers} 次</small></article>`}).join('')}

// SOURCE: save-system.js
(() => {
  'use strict';
  const balance=globalThis.BALANCE_V21,manifest=globalThis.PERSONA_BALANCE_MANIFEST;
  if(!balance||!manifest)throw new Error('Run save requires balance manifest');
  const SAVE_KEY='persona-run-save-v3',TEMP_KEY='persona-run-save-temp-v3',LEGACY_KEYS=['persona-run-save-v2','persona-run-save-temp-v2','persona-run-save-v1','persona-run-save-temp-v1'],VERSION=3;
  const phases=['stage_intro','boss_reveal','battle','settlement','route','event','shop','report','forge','choice','acquire','persona_growth','slice_end','target_loadout','target_report','target_carry'];
  let invalidatedLegacySave=false;
  const parse=raw=>{try{return JSON.parse(raw)}catch{return null}};
  const validCard=card=>card&&typeof card==='object'&&typeof card.r==='string'&&typeof card.s==='string'&&Number.isFinite(card.ri)&&(!card.shopModifiers||(['bonusCoins','bonusMult','bonusXmultRate'].every(key=>Number.isFinite(Number(card.shopModifiers[key]||0)))));
  function validShopSession(runState){
    const node=manifest.stageNodes.find(item=>item.id===runState?.currentNodeId),session=runState?.nodeRuntimeById?.[runState.currentNodeId]?.data?.shopSession;if(!session)return true;
    const profile=manifest.shop?.refreshProfiles?.find(item=>item.id===session.profileId),items=new Map((manifest.shop?.items||[]).map(item=>[item.id,item]));if(!profile||node?.shopProfileId&&node.shopProfileId!==profile.id||session.version!==2||!Number.isInteger(session.refreshIndex)||session.refreshIndex<0||!Array.isArray(session.offers)||session.offers.length>profile.offerSlotCount||!Array.isArray(session.purchasedItemIds))return false;
    if(new Set(session.purchasedItemIds).size!==session.purchasedItemIds.length||session.purchasedItemIds.some(id=>!items.has(id)))return false;
    const offerIds=new Set(),itemIds=new Set();for(const offer of session.offers){const item=items.get(offer.itemId);if(!item||typeof offer.offerId!=='string'||offerIds.has(offer.offerId)||itemIds.has(offer.itemId)||!Number.isInteger(offer.purchaseCount)||offer.purchaseCount<0||offer.purchaseCount>item.purchaseLimit)return false;offerIds.add(offer.offerId);itemIds.add(offer.itemId)}return session.selectedItemId==null||itemIds.has(session.selectedItemId);
  }
  function validRunState(runState){
    const template=manifest.runTemplates.find(item=>item.id===runState?.runTemplateId);if(!template||!template.nodeIds.includes(runState.currentNodeId))return false;
    if(!['NOT_ENTERED','ENTERED','IN_PROGRESS','COMPLETED','RUN_COMPLETED'].includes(runState.nodeStatus))return false;
    if(!Array.isArray(runState.completedNodeIds)||runState.completedNodeIds.some(id=>!template.nodeIds.includes(id)))return false;
    const templateBattleCount=template.nodeIds.map(id=>manifest.stageNodes.find(node=>node.id===id)).filter(node=>node?.type==='BATTLE').length;
    if(!Number.isInteger(runState.battleIndexCompat)||runState.battleIndexCompat<0||runState.battleIndexCompat>=templateBattleCount||!Number.isInteger(runState.transitionVersion))return false;
    const equipped=runState.equippedPersonaInstanceIds||[null,null,null,null],instances=runState.personaInstancesById||{},templates=new Set([...(manifest.personaTemplates?.templates||[]).map(item=>item.id),...Object.keys(runState.dynamicPersonaTemplatesById||{})]);
    const growth=runState.shopGrowthState||{},validGrowthMap=map=>map&&typeof map==='object'&&!Array.isArray(map)&&Object.values(map).every(value=>Number.isFinite(value)&&value>=0),developerOptions=runState.developerOptions||{},directions=runState.aiPersonaDirectionByNode||{},validDirections=directions&&typeof directions==='object'&&!Array.isArray(directions)&&Object.entries(directions).every(([nodeId,directionId])=>['N04','N08','N12'].includes(nodeId)&&['AI_DIRECTION_BRIDGE','AI_DIRECTION_BREAK','AI_DIRECTION_FOLLOW'].includes(directionId));
    return equipped.length===4&&new Set(equipped.filter(Boolean)).size===equipped.filter(Boolean).length&&equipped.filter(Boolean).every(id=>instances[id])&&Object.values(instances).every(instance=>templates.has(instance.templateId))&&validGrowthMap(growth.suitChipBonusBySuit||{})&&validGrowthMap(growth.suitLevelsBySuit||{})&&validGrowthMap(growth.handTypeLevelsById||{})&&(developerOptions.offlineAiPersonaEnabled===undefined||typeof developerOptions.offlineAiPersonaEnabled==='boolean')&&(runState.personaGenerationMode===undefined||['LOCAL_V1','LEGACY_PROTOTYPE','DISABLED'].includes(runState.personaGenerationMode))&&validDirections&&validShopSession(runState);
  }
  function validate(save){
    if(!save||save.version!==VERSION||!save.state||!phases.includes(save.phase)||!validRunState(save.state.runState))return false;const state=save.state;
    if(!Number.isInteger(state.battleIndex)||state.battleIndex!==state.runState.battleIndexCompat||!Number.isFinite(state.coins)||state.coins<0)return false;
    if(!Array.isArray(state.hand)||state.hand.length>balance.battle.startingHandSize||!state.hand.every(validCard))return false;
    return Array.isArray(state.deck)&&state.deck.every(validCard)&&Array.isArray(state.runDeck)&&state.runDeck.every(validCard);
  }
  function persist(save){try{const raw=JSON.stringify(save);localStorage.setItem(TEMP_KEY,raw);if(!validate(parse(localStorage.getItem(TEMP_KEY))))return false;localStorage.setItem(SAVE_KEY,raw);localStorage.removeItem(TEMP_KEY);return true}catch{return false}}
  function read(){
    for(const key of [SAVE_KEY,TEMP_KEY]){const save=parse(localStorage.getItem(key));if(validate(save))return save}
    for(const key of [SAVE_KEY,TEMP_KEY,...LEGACY_KEYS]){if(localStorage.getItem(key)!==null){localStorage.removeItem(key);invalidatedLegacySave=true}}
    return null;
  }
  function write(phase,reason='auto'){if(typeof window.buildRunSaveState!=='function')return false;const save={version:VERSION,savedAt:Date.now(),phase,reason,state:window.buildRunSaveState()};if(!validate(save)||!persist(save))return false;window.dispatchEvent?.(new CustomEvent('run-save-changed'));return true}
  function clear(){try{for(const key of [SAVE_KEY,TEMP_KEY,...LEGACY_KEYS])localStorage.removeItem(key);invalidatedLegacySave=false;window.dispatchEvent?.(new CustomEvent('run-save-changed'))}catch{}}
  function restore(){const save=read();return save&&typeof window.restoreRunSaveState==='function'?window.restoreRunSaveState(save):false}
  function summary(){const save=read();if(!save)return null;const labels={stage_intro:'关卡揭示',boss_reveal:'关卡揭示',battle:'战斗中',settlement:'战斗结算',route:'路线选择',event:'随机事件',shop:'幕间商店',report:'人格报告',forge:'人格铸造',choice:'人格三选一',acquire:'人格获得',persona_growth:'人格成长测试',slice_end:'垂直切片结束',target_loadout:'目标长局人格选择',target_report:'目标长局报告',target_carry:'目标长局人格带出'};return{...save,label:labels[save.phase]||save.phase,node:save.state.runState.battleIndexCompat+1,currentNodeId:save.state.runState.currentNodeId}}
  function hadInvalidatedLegacySave(){read();return invalidatedLegacySave}
  window.runSave={read,write,clear,restore,summary,validate,hadInvalidatedLegacySave,key:SAVE_KEY};window.commitRunSave=(phase,reason)=>write(phase,reason);window.clearRunSave=clear;
})();


// SOURCE: tutorial.js
(() => {
  const KEY='persona-tutorial-complete-v1';
  const steps=[
    {target:'.run-score',title:'完成本关目标',copy:'在出牌次数耗尽前，让当前得分达到右侧目标分数。每一场战斗都有独立目标。',label:'目标与胜负'},
    {target:'.hand-zone',title:'选择你的手牌',copy:'点击手牌选择或取消，单手最多打出 5 张。可按点数或花色重新排序，不会改变牌库。',label:'选择与排序'},
    {target:'.score-preview',title:'组合决定得分',copy:'上方依次显示牌型、基础筹码、倍率和最终得分。左下角“牌型规则”可随时查看完整表格。',label:'牌型计分'},
    {target:'.persona-rail',title:'人格会回应选择',copy:'左侧装备的人格牌会在满足条件时为本手增加筹码、倍率或独立倍率。',label:'人格触发'}
  ];
  let index=0,active=false,returnToSettings=false;
  const overlay=document.querySelector('#tutorial-overlay'),focus=overlay.querySelector('.tutorial-focus'),card=overlay.querySelector('.tutorial-card');
  function position(){
    document.querySelectorAll('.tutorial-target').forEach(node=>node.classList.remove('tutorial-target'));
    const target=document.querySelector(steps[index].target);if(!target)return;target.classList.add('tutorial-target');
    const rect=target.getBoundingClientRect(),pad=8;focus.style.left=`${Math.max(8,rect.left-pad)}px`;focus.style.top=`${Math.max(8,rect.top-pad)}px`;focus.style.width=`${Math.min(innerWidth-16,rect.width+pad*2)}px`;focus.style.height=`${Math.min(innerHeight-16,rect.height+pad*2)}px`;
    const width=Math.min(430,innerWidth*.88),below=rect.bottom+18,top=below+210<innerHeight?below:Math.max(14,rect.top-224);card.style.left=`${Math.max(14,Math.min(innerWidth-width-14,rect.left+rect.width/2-width/2))}px`;card.style.top=`${top}px`;
  }
  function render(){const step=steps[index];document.querySelector('#tutorial-step-label').textContent=`${String(index+1).padStart(2,'0')} / ${String(steps.length).padStart(2,'0')} · ${step.label}`;document.querySelector('#tutorial-title').textContent=step.title;document.querySelector('#tutorial-copy').textContent=step.copy;document.querySelector('#tutorial-prev').disabled=index===0;document.querySelector('#tutorial-next').textContent=index===steps.length-1?'完成教学':'下一步';position()}
  function open(force=false){if(!force&&localStorage.getItem(KEY)==='1')return;active=true;index=0;overlay.classList.remove('hidden');render();window.gameSfx?.('button')}
  function close(completed=true){active=false;overlay.classList.add('hidden');document.querySelectorAll('.tutorial-target').forEach(node=>node.classList.remove('tutorial-target'));if(completed)localStorage.setItem(KEY,'1');if(returnToSettings){returnToSettings=false;window.openSettingsFromTutorial?.()}}
  function next(){if(index<steps.length-1){index++;render();window.gameSfx?.('button')}else close(true)}
  document.querySelector('#tutorial-next').onclick=next;document.querySelector('#tutorial-prev').onclick=()=>{if(index){index--;render();window.gameSfx?.('button')}};document.querySelector('#tutorial-skip').onclick=()=>close(true);
  document.querySelector('#replay-tutorial').onclick=()=>{returnToSettings=true;window.closeSettingsForTutorial?.();setTimeout(()=>open(true),50)};
  addEventListener('resize',()=>active&&position());addEventListener('keydown',event=>{if(!active)return;event.stopImmediatePropagation();if(event.key==='Escape'){event.preventDefault();close(true)}if(event.key==='ArrowRight'||event.key==='Enter'){event.preventDefault();next()}if(event.key==='ArrowLeft'&&index){event.preventDefault();index--;render()}},true);
  window.maybeOpenBattleTutorial=()=>setTimeout(()=>open(false),180);
  window.battleTutorial={open,close,get active(){return active},get index(){return index},steps};
})();
